# The Lycanthropic Curse
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Haunted place
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Unity415}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a remote, haunted wetland, notorious pirate Amber and her loyal first mate Pepper seek hidden treasure, only to uncover an ancient curse that plagues the land. They must navigate eerie landscapes, face supernatural threats, and confront the mysterious Silver, leader of a cursed tribe of lycanthropic elves. The adventurers' cunning and bravery will be tested as they work to break the curse or succumb to the horrors of the wetlands.
### Hook
The players, independently renowned for their various skills, receive word of a hidden treasure buried in a haunted wetland, a place avoided by locals due to tales of an ancient curse. Driven by curiosity, greed, or the thrill of adventure, they converge on the eerie landscape, ready to uncover its secrets and claim the treasure.
### Location Descriptions
1. **The Haunted Wetland Entrance**: A misty, foreboding swamp entrance, where twisted trees and eerie sounds set a chilling tone for the adventure.
2. **Ghostly Battlefield**: A clearing where the players first encounter the spectral creatures, with remnants of past battles and eerie, glowing mist.
3. **Hermit's Hut**: A decrepit, vine-covered shack on a small island, containing cryptic journals and clues about the curse and the treasure.
4. **Silver's Encampment**: Hidden deep in the swamp, this camp is home to Silver and her tribe, blending into the landscape with natural camouflage.
5. **Ancient Submerged Temple**: The heart of the wetlands, this crumbling structure is partially submerged, filled with traps, puzzles, and the final cursed guardian protecting the powerful artefact.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As Amber and Pepper, along with their new companions, venture deeper into the wetlands, they are attacked by a pack of twisted, ghostly creatures. These spectral beings are the vengeful spirits of those who previously sought the treasure and fell victim to the curse. The players must defend themselves and find a safe place to regroup.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After fending off the spirits, the players explore the wetlands, encountering eerie landmarks and remnants of past expeditions. They find clues hinting at the nature of the curse and the treasure's location. A decrepit hut belonging to a hermit holds vital information about the tribe of lycanthropic elves and their mysterious leader, Silver.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they follow the clues, the players are ambushed by members of Silver's tribe, who warn them to leave. They are taken to Silver, who reveals her tragic past and the curse afflicting her people. Silver offers a reluctant alliance, suggesting that the only way to lift the curse is to retrieve a powerful artefact from the heart of the wetlands. However, she withholds crucial information, testing the players' trust.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
With Silver's partial guidance, the players navigate to the artefact's location, a crumbling, ancient temple submerged in the swamp. Inside, they face off against a powerful, cursed guardian that protects the artefact. The confrontation is intense, requiring all their cunning, bravery, and teamwork to defeat the guardian and claim the artefact.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the artefact in hand, the players return to Silver. They have a choice: use the artefact to lift the curse from the wetlands and the lycanthropic tribe or keep it for themselves, potentially gaining great power but dooming the land and its inhabitants to eternal torment. Their decision shapes the final outcome of the adventure.

### Endings
1. **Curse Lifted**: The players use the artefact to lift the curse. The wetlands begin to heal, and the spirits of the cursed find peace. Silver and her tribe are freed from their lycanthropy, and the players receive a trove of treasure as a reward.
2. **Artefact Kept**: The players keep the artefact, gaining its power but leaving the curse intact. The wetlands grow more dangerous, and the tribe remains cursed. The players leave with their newfound power but are haunted by the consequences of their greed.
3. **Artefact Destroyed**: The players decide to destroy the artefact, preventing anyone from using its power. The wetlands remain cursed, but the immediate threat is gone. The tribe remains lycanthropic, but Silver thanks them for their effort, offering a modest reward and safe passage out of the wetlands.{{pageNumber,auto}}

![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# Specters in the Ice
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Haunted place
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Manassé}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the desolate reaches of the Frozen Land, a fortress long abandoned by time and its creators holds a dark secret. Fjotra Boulderchest, a once brave and now overconfident dwarf siege engineer, has unwittingly awoken an ancient curse within its icy walls. Adventurers must brave the chilling landscape, confront spectral horrors, and unravel the mystery to end the fortress's haunting.
### Hook
The players are drawn to the Frozen Land by rumours of a dwarven fortress, Frostbane, containing untold treasures and powerful artefacts. However, recent tales speak of eerie occurrences and the disappearance of those who venture near. A local elder implores the players to investigate and put an end to the fortress's curse.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Frozen Outskirts**: The desolate, icy perimeter of Frostbane Fortress where the players meet the survivor and face the first signs of the haunting.
2. **Outer Courtyard**: An open area filled with abandoned siege equipment and scattered remnants of the dwarven engineers' last stand.
3. **Hall of Engineers:** A room shrouded in mystery, it contains blueprints, journals, and notes detailing Fjotra's work and the ill-fated ritual. What secrets lie within these pages?
4. **Defensive Corridors**: Narrow, winding passages filled with traps and ancient defences triggered by Fjotra's spectral presence.
5. **Grand Hall:** The heart of the fortress, a grand and imposing space where the players confront Fjotra and disrupt the ritual to lift the curse. This is the epic climax of your journey.
6. **Treasure Vault**: A hidden chamber revealed after the curse is lifted, containing the dwarven engineers' valuable artefacts and treasures.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon reaching the outskirts of Frostbane Fortress, the players encounter a distraught and frostbitten survivor who escaped the haunted stronghold. He warns them of ghostly apparitions and strange noises emanating from within, setting the stage for the players' perilous journey inside.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The players navigate the fortress's frozen halls, discovering signs of a past dwarven siege and abandoned equipment designed by Fjotra. They find journals and notes that hint at a forgotten ritual performed by Fjotra to protect the fortress, which instead unleashed the curse. The players must piece together the history and purpose of the ritual.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the players delve deeper, they encounter Fjotra herself, now a spectral entity driven by overconfidence and desperation. She mistakes the players for intruders and triggers ancient siege defences, complicating their exploration. The players must convince Fjotra of their intentions or disable the defences while avoiding her attacks.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players reach the heart of the fortress, a grand hall where the ritual occurred. Here, they must confront Fjotra's spectral form in a final battle, navigating her engineering traps and supernatural abilities. To succeed, they must disrupt the ritual's power source and defeat Fjotra, putting her restless spirit to peace.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Fjotra defeated and the curse lifted, the fortress begins to thaw. The players uncover the hidden treasures and powerful artefacts of the dwarven engineers. As they leave Frostbane Fortress, the land around it shows signs of life returning, indicating their success.

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column
### Endings
1. **Triumphant Ending**: The players defeat Fjotra and lift the curse, finding valuable treasures and receiving accolades from the local community. The Frozen Land begins to thaw, hinting at future prosperity.
2. **Bittersweet Ending**: The players lift the curse but at the cost of one or more of their members. They find some treasure, but the land remains harsh and unforgiving, a reminder of the sacrifices made.
3. **Tragic Ending**: The players fail to lift the curse or are overwhelmed by Fjotra and her traps. The fortress remains haunted, and the Frozen Land stays desolate. Survivors might escape with their lives but nothing more.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# The Ensnaring Melody
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Haunted place
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Justin Stoddard}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of the sinister Darkwood Forest, an ancient curse lies dormant, waiting to ensnare unsuspecting souls. Marsan, a charismatic and loyal blacksmith, is drawn into this dark mystery along with his melancholic and wise companion, Nuri, and the adventurous and jovial warrior, Ryson. As they venture deeper into the haunted forest, they uncover the malevolent influence of a vengeful dwarf musician named Cuurple, whose cursed melody holds the key to the forest's sinister power.
### Hook
The players, independent adventurers in a nearby village, are approached by Marsan, a charismatic blacksmith with a personal vendetta. He seeks their aid in lifting a curse that has plagued Darkwood Forest for generations. Marsan's brother is taken by the forest's dark power, and he vows to rescue him and end the curse once and for all. The promise of ancient treasures hidden in the forest entices the adventurers to join Marsan, Nuri, and Ryson on their perilous journey.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Darkwood Village:** A small, secluded village on the edge of Darkwood Forest. The villagers live in constant fear of the forest's curse.
2. **Darkwood Forest Entrance:** The foreboding entrance to the cursed forest, with twisted trees and an eerie atmosphere sets the tone for the adventure.
3. **Abandoned Campsites:** Scattered throughout the forest, these sites provide clues about previous adventurers and the dangers that lurk within.
4. **Ancient Ruins:** Crumbling remnants of a bygone era, offering hidden secrets and important clues about the forest's dark history.
5. **Druid's Grove:** A hidden sanctuary within the forest where a reclusive druid offers wisdom and warnings to the players.
6. **Forest Tribe's Village:** A small settlement of forest-dwelling creatures who share tales of the cursed melody and the forest's dark power.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the party enters Darkwood Forest, they are immediately confronted by eerie whispers and strange, haunting melodies that seem to emanate from the very trees. The forest itself appears alive, with twisted branches that reach out like skeletal hands. Suddenly, shadowy figures emerge, the cursed remnants of previous adventurers who fell victim to the forest's curse. The players must fend off these ghostly attackers while trying to keep their wits about them.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After overcoming the initial attack, the players begin to explore the forest. They come across abandoned campsites, ancient ruins, and strange markings on the trees. They also encounter various inhabitants of the forest, including a reclusive druid who warns them of the forest's dark history and a tribe of forest-dwelling creatures who share tales of a cursed melody that drives men mad. Clues point towards an ancient, forgotten shrine deep within the forest where the source of the curse lies.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they delve deeper, the players discover that Cuurple, a once-gifted dwarf musician, is the source of the cursed melody. Cuurple was betrayed by his own kin and cursed by dark magic, transforming his sorrowful tunes into deadly enchantments. The players find themselves in a race against time as Cuurple's minions, drawn by the power of his music, hunt them relentlessly. The forest itself seems to conspire against them, with paths shifting and illusions leading them astray. They must navigate these dangers while deciphering the clues to locate Cuurple's lair.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players finally reach the ancient shrine, a decaying structure covered in vines and dark symbols. Inside, they find Cuurple playing his cursed melody on a sinister-looking instrument. A fierce battle ensues, with the players having to overcome not only Cuurple's powerful magic but also the animated forest and Cuurple's minions. They must disrupt the cursed melody and defeat Cuurple to break the curse and free the forest from its dark grip.

#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Cuurple defeated and the cursed melody silenced, the forest begins to transform. The twisted trees regain their natural form, and the haunting whispers fade away. Marsan finds his brother among the freed spirits, allowing him a moment of closure. The players are celebrated as heroes in the nearby village, receiving their promised rewards and the gratitude of those saved from the curse.
### Endings
1. **Heroic Ending:** The players successfully defeat Cuurple and lift the curse. The forest is restored to its natural beauty, and Marsan is reunited with his brother's spirit, bringing him peace. The village rewards the players with treasures and a place of honour in their community.
2. **Bittersweet Ending:** The players manage to defeat Cuurple but at a great cost. Some of their allies are lost or severely wounded, and the forest remains scarred by the curse's influence. Marsan finds his brother's spirit but is haunted by the knowledge that he couldn't save him in life.
3. **Tragic Ending:** The players fail to defeat Cuurple, and the curse continues to plague the forest. The players barely escape with their lives, and the village mourns the loss of hope. Marsan is left consumed by vengeance, and the forest remains a place of dread and darkness.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Hollow Hill
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Haunted place
}}
::

{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
Hollow Hill, once a serene and idyllic place, has become a site of terror where dark whispers of restless spirits and eerie phenomena have spread fear among the local population. Drawn by duty and a gnawing curiosity, Countess Korga Giantbane, a half-orc noble, sets out to uncover the truth behind the haunted hill. She is joined by her loyal gnome general, Razzle Yarnspinner, and a group of adventurers who have answered her call for aid. The true source of the hill's curse lies in the betrayal of Roxana Havoc, a tiefling fletcher who was once an ally. As the players delve into the mysteries of Hollow Hill, they must confront not only supernatural forces but also the dark secrets that bind them to this place.
### Hook
The players, a group of experienced adventurers, receive a summons from Countess Korga Giantbane, a respected half-orc noble known for her diplomatic skills and pious nature. She needs their help to investigate the mysterious happenings at Hollow Hill, a place now feared by the locals due to recent ghostly encounters and the sudden disappearance of a child. Korga offers them a generous reward and the promise of her favoعr should they assist her in uncovering the truth behind the haunting. The task seems straightforward, but as they venture closer to the hill, they quickly realise that something far more sinister is at play.
### Location Descriptions
1. **The Village of Hollow Hill:** A small, frightened community at the base of the hill. The villagers are on edge, haunted by the recent disappearances and strange occurrences. This is where the players first gather information and meet the locals.
2. **The Fields of Echoing Wails:** The fields surrounding Hollow Hill, where the hauntings are most intense. The fields are covered in thick fog, with ghostly shapes often glimpsed in the distance. It’s here that the players find clues about Roxana’s fate and the nature of the curse.
3. **Roxana’s Cabin:** The abandoned home of Roxana Havoc, located on the lower slopes of the hill. The cabin is filled with fletching tools, old notes, and personal effects that provide insights into her life and tragic death. This is also where the players may first encounter her spirit.
4. **The Shrine of Betrayal:** A hidden shrine near the summit of Hollow Hill, where the players discover the truth about Roxana’s betrayal. The shrine is tainted by dark magic, and it’s here that the final confrontation with both Roxana and the dark entity takes place.
5. **The Heart of the Hill:** The epicentre of the haunting, a place where the boundary between the living and the dead is thin. The heart of the hill is a nightmarish landscape of twisted trees, unnatural shadows, and the pervasive sense of being watched. It is here that the final battle to break the curse occurs.

::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players, along with Korga and Razzle, arrive at the base of Hollow Hill, they are met with a scene of chaos. Villagers are gathered in desperation, frantically searching for a missing child who was last seen near the cursed fields. The locals claim that the spirits of the hill have taken the child as an offering. The players must decide how to handle the panicked crowd and whether to investigate the missing child immediately or first gather more information about the hauntings.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The players investigate a haunting that began after the death of local fletcher Roxana Havoc, a close ally of Korga. They find strange sightings, ghostly wails, and an unnatural fog in the hill and fields. They discover ritualistic symbols, a hidden shrine, and clues suggesting Roxana's death might not have been natural. Their deeper investigation reveals Roxana's spirit may be linked to the hill's curse.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Players discover Roxana's ghost reveals she was betrayed and her spirit haunts a hill seeking justice. The hill is also under the influence of a dark entity that manipulates Roxana's anger. The players must decide whether to cleanse Roxana's spirit through combat or convince her to move on. The dark entity intensifies hauntings, threatening to trap the players forever on the hill.

\column

#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players confront Roxana’s spirit at the heart of Hollow Hill, where the boundaries between the material and spiritual worlds are weakest. Depending on their choices, this confrontation could be a battle against her enraged ghost or a tense negotiation to help her find peace. However, the dark entity lurking within the hill also reveals itself, challenging the players to a final showdown. They must overcome both the vengeful spirit and the malevolent force driving the curse if they hope to free Hollow Hill from its grip.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the defeat of the dark entity and the resolution of Roxana’s spirit, the curse on Hollow Hill is lifted. The fog dissipates, the wails cease, and the missing child is found safe, though frightened, at the edge of the woods. Korga thanks the players for their bravery and offers them the promised reward, along with her eternal gratitude. The villagers begin to return to their normal lives, and Hollow Hill is restored to its former peaceful state—though it will always bear the scars of the tragedy that once befell it.

### Endings
1. **Peace Restored:** If the players successfully convince Roxana to move on peacefully and defeat the dark entity, they are celebrated as heroes. They receive Korga’s reward, along with a magical item imbued with the residual energy of Hollow Hill, such as a Ghostlight Lantern that can reveal hidden truths.
2. **Vengeance Fulfilled:** If the players destroy Roxana’s spirit in combat, they break the curse but at a cost. The hill remains eerie and scarred, and Korga is saddened by the loss of her former ally. The players receive their reward, but with a sombre tone, as they are reminded of the cost of violence.
3. **Darkness Prevails:** If the players fail to stop the dark entity, Hollow Hill remains cursed. The players barely escape with their lives, and Korga is forced to abandon the area, condemning the villagers to live in fear. They receive only a fraction of the reward, as the mission is deemed a failure.{{pageNumber,auto}}

:::::
# Wraiths of the Forgotten
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Haunted place
}}
::

{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the midst of an endless night, Natalia Hawthorne, a skilled necromancer, and her jovial dwarf companion, Vonana Mountainson, are drawn to Noblecrest Hill. In this cursed location, the dead do not rest. Delilah Noblecrest, a local banker, has unwittingly triggered the wrath of vengeful spirits while attempting to uncover ancient, forbidden knowledge. Natalia and Vonana must unravel the secrets of the haunted hill, face the restless dead, and confront Delilah before the spirits consume her and spread their curse beyond the hill.
### Hook
The players, travelling together for their reasons, find themselves in a small village near Noblecrest Hill. As they prepare to rest for the night, an old woman approaches them, desperate for help. She claims that Delilah Noblecrest, the local banker, has ventured up the haunted hill in search of something and hasn't returned. The villagers are too afraid to follow, as Noblecrest Hill is known to be cursed—those who go up often don’t come back. The players are implored to investigate, with the promise of a hefty reward if they bring Delilah back alive.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Noblecrest Hill:** A steep, desolate hill shrouded in fog. The trees are twisted and gnarled, and the ground is uneven and rocky. The atmosphere is thick with the presence of restless spirits, and the air is unnaturally cold.
2. **The Battlefield Ruins:** Halfway up the hill, the players find the remnants of an ancient battle. Skeletons and broken weapons are scattered around, and ghostly figures drift among the debris, eternally reliving their last moments.
3. **The Hidden Crypt:** Beneath the hill, accessible through a concealed entrance in the ruins, lies a crypt built centuries ago. The walls are lined with skulls, and the air is thick with the smell of decay. This is where the necromancer’s phylactery is kept, surrounded by dark magic and the spirits of those who died trying to stop him.
4. **Delilah’s Carriage:** The players find Delilah’s overturned carriage near the base of the hill. The horses are gone, and the carriage itself shows signs of a violent struggle. This is where the players first encounter the hostile spirits.
5. **The Village:** A small, quiet village at the foot of Noblecrest Hill. The villagers are superstitious and fearful, with most refusing to speak of the hill after dark. The old woman who seeks the players' help lives here, her house filled with protective charms and symbols.


::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players ascend Noblecrest Hill, the oppressive atmosphere grows thicker with each step. A dense fog surrounds them, and the air becomes colder, carrying whispers of the restless dead. The players soon find Delilah’s carriage overturned and abandoned, with signs of a struggle leading further up the hill. They must fend off a group of ghastly spirits intent on keeping them from advancing further.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Exploring the hill further, the players come across remnants of an ancient battlefield. Broken weapons and armour litter the ground, and the spirits of fallen soldiers linger in torment. Among these ruins, they discover an old, tattered journal belonging to Delilah. The journal reveals that she was searching for an ancient artefact rumoured to grant immense power, but she didn't realise the artefact was cursed—it's what binds the spirits to this place. They also find evidence of a hidden crypt beneath the hill, where the artefact is likely kept.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they approach the entrance to the crypt, a terrifying wail echoes through the air. The fog thickens, and the players are ambushed by spectral knights—the most powerful of the spirits trapped here. These knights are not just mindless undead; they are driven by the same curiosity that led Delilah to this place and can speak to the players, revealing more about the curse and its origins. The players must decide whether to fight their way through or try to negotiate with the knights, possibly gaining valuable allies or information.

## Vonana Mountainson

\column
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Within the crypt, they find Delilah, nearly possessed by the malevolent spirit of an ancient necromancer who was entombed here long ago. The artefact she sought is revealed to be the necromancer’s phylactery, holding a fragment of his soul. The players must face off against Delilah, who is now partially under the necromancer's control while dealing with the lingering spirits in the crypt. They must destroy the phylactery to break the curse and free the spirits, or they risk unleashing the necromancer’s full power.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
If the players destroy the phylactery, the spirits are finally laid to rest, and Delilah is freed from the necromancer’s influence. The hill returns to its eerie silence, but the curse is lifted, and the villagers are safe. If they fail, the necromancer is fully resurrected, possessing Delilah’s body entirely and vowing to spread his influence beyond the hill. Either way, the players must decide what to do with Delilah—whether she lives, dies, or is left to her fate.
::
### Endings
1. **The Spirits’ Release:** The players destroy the phylactery, banishing the necromancer's soul and freeing the spirits trapped on the hill. Delilah, now herself again, is grateful and offers the players a portion of her wealth as thanks. The villagers celebrate the players as heroes, and the hill becomes a place of peace, no longer feared.
2. **Delilah’s Downfall:** The players fail to destroy the phylactery, and the necromancer fully possesses Delilah. She escapes, now a vessel for the necromancer’s evil, and disappears into the night. The players must choose whether to pursue her or warn the villagers of the impending doom. The hill remains cursed, and the players leave with a sense of foreboding.
3. **The Compromise:** The players manage to weaken the necromancer’s hold on Delilah but do not fully destroy the phylactery. Delilah is left in a fragile state, half herself and half under the necromancer’s influence. She pleads with the players to take the phylactery far away, where its power can never be unleashed. The players gain a powerful, albeit dangerous, artefact but leave with the knowledge that the curse may one day return.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Bane of the Warlock
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Callum Morris}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the shadow of the Whispering Peaks, an ancient curse has been reawakened. Proserpine Bane, a melancholic yet cunning Tiefling Warlock, and Rusk Landslide, a charismatic but impulsive Half-Orc Gatherer, must navigate a labyrinthine cave system to confront the enigmatic jeweller, Sog Mudguard. The adventurers must brave supernatural horrors and unravel the dark history of the mountain to break the curse.
### Hook
The players are drawn to the Whispering Peaks by rumours of a powerful artefact hidden deep within the mountain. Proserpine and Rusk, driven by their own motivations—Proserpine's pursuit of forbidden knowledge and Rusk's desire to protect their homeland—team up to explore the treacherous caves. However, they soon discover that the artefact is linked to an ancient curse that has begun to spread its influence, affecting nearby villages and the mountain itself.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Whispering Peaks Village**: A small, isolated settlement at the base of the mountain, plagued by supernatural occurrences and inhabited by fearful but hopeful villagers.
2. **Labyrinthine Cave Entrance**: The foreboding entrance to the cave system, marked by ancient runes and warnings of the curse within.
3. **Ancient Wards Chamber**: A series of rooms filled with traps, puzzles, and remnants of ancient magical wards designed to contain the curse.
4. **Hidden Chamber of Spirits**: A secret room haunted by the restless spirits of the ancient civilisation, offering cryptic advice and a moral dilemma.
5. **Echoing Cavern**: The final confrontation site, a vast cavern illuminated by the gem's eerie glow, where the players face Sog Mudguard and the cave's supernatural guardians.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon reaching the village at the base of the Whispering Peaks, the players find it in disarray. The villagers speak of haunting whispers, eerie lights emanating from the caves, and strange, supernatural occurrences. An elder informs Proserpine and Rusk that these phenomena started when a jeweller named Sog Mudguard began exploring the caves, seeking a legendary gem said to grant immense power. The villagers beg the adventurers to investigate and stop Sog before it's too late.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Entering the labyrinthine cave system, the players encounter a series of traps and puzzles, remnants of ancient wards meant to contain the curse. They discover eerie carvings and journals left by previous explorers, revealing the history of the curse: an ancient civilisation once thrived within the mountain, but their greed and misuse of the gem led to their downfall. As they delve deeper, the players find evidence of Sog's passage, including discarded mining tools and traces of arcane rituals.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Deep within the cave, the players stumble upon a hidden chamber filled with the restless spirits of the ancient civilisation. These spirits, tormented by their eternal imprisonment, present a moral dilemma: to free them, the players must destroy the artefact, but doing so could also doom Sog and any hope of lifting the curse. The spirits offer cryptic advice and visions, hinting at a secret passage that leads directly to Sog's current location.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Navigating the secret passage, the players confront Sog Mudguard in a vast, echoing cavern illuminated by the gem's eerie glow. Sog, now twisted by the gem's power, refuses to relinquish it, believing he can control its power. A fierce battle ensues, with the players facing not only Sog but also the cave's supernatural guardians awakened by the artefact's activation. They must use their wits and newfound knowledge to overcome these threats and either seize or destroy the gem.

\column
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Sog defeated and the gem either claimed or destroyed, the cave begins to collapse as the ancient magic unravels. The players must make a harrowing escape, navigating the collapsing tunnels and avoiding falling debris. Once outside, they find the village beginning to return to normal as the curse lifts. The villagers thank them, offering rewards and pledging their eternal gratitude.
::
### Endings
1. **Heroic Ending**: The players destroy the gem, freeing the spirits and lifting the curse. The village celebrates their bravery, and they are hailed as heroes. Proserpine gains valuable knowledge from the ancient texts, while Rusk earns the villagers' respect and friendship.
2. **Selfish Ending**: The players claim the gem for themselves, suppressing the curse's effects but leaving the spirits trapped. They gain the gem's power, but rumours of their actions spread, attracting unwanted attention from powerful enemies.
3. **Tragic Ending**: The players fail to stop Sog, and the curse spreads uncontrollably. The village is abandoned, and the players barely escape with their lives. Haunted by their failure, they are driven to seek redemption in future adventures.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page

# The Mirthmech Chronicles
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Clary}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of an uncharted jungle, the party is tasked with uncovering the secrets of the fabled Lost City of Zuthara. Zenki Tenders, a jovial and wise Gnome Illusionist, leads the expedition alongside his cunning Tiefling rogue companion Nathiri and the pious yet impulsive Gnome scout Daphne Mirthmech. Their adventure becomes perilous when they encounter the cunning and sceptical mercenary captain Wrenn Inventoria, whose stubbornness poses a significant threat. The journey will test their resolve, unravel ancient mysteries, and reveal hidden dangers within the jungle.
### Hook
The party receives a request from Zenki Tenders, a respected wizard searching for lost knowledge, to join him on an expedition to the mysterious jungle. Rumours of an ancient city filled with untold treasures and forgotten lore have surfaced, and Zenki needs skilled adventurers to help uncover its secrets. Intrigued by the promise of adventure and discovery, the players agree to join him.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Jungle Periphery**: The dense, vibrant outskirts of the jungle where the party begins their journey, teeming with exotic flora and fauna.
2. **Ancient Ruins**: The scattered remains of Zuthara's outer structures, where the party finds the first clues and faces initial puzzles.
3. **Central Temple:** The heart of the Lost City, a grand and foreboding structure of immense size and beauty, filled with traps, guardians, and the most significant secrets of Zuthara.
4. **Hidden Catacombs**: Underground passages beneath the city containing ancient artefacts and the remnants of the city's once-great civilisation.
5. **Mercenary Camp**: The temporary base of Wrenn Inventoria and his crew, strategically located near the central temple, posing a constant threat to the party's progress.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the party ventures deeper into the jungle, they face the initial conflict: surviving the treacherous terrain and the natural hazards of the jungle. From venomous creatures to unpredictable weather, the journey to the rumoured site of Zuthara is fraught with danger. The party must use their skills and wits to navigate these challenges and protect each other.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Upon reaching the outskirts of the Lost City of Zuthara, the party begins their exploration. They uncover ancient ruins, decipher cryptic inscriptions, and find clues that point towards the heart of the city. Along the way, they encounter remnants of the city's former inhabitants and pieces of powerful artefacts. This phase involves a mix of puzzle-solving and social interactions with any remaining guardians or spirits of the city.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The party's progress is interrupted by the arrival of Wrenn Inventoria and his mercenary crew, who have also been drawn to the city by the promise of its treasures. Wrenn's cunning and sceptical nature makes him a formidable adversary. He believes the party has valuable information about the city's secrets and decides to capture them. The players must find a way to outwit Wrenn and his mercenaries, either through stealth, negotiation, or direct confrontation.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The adventure's climax takes place deep within the central temple of Zuthara. Here, the party faces the final confrontation against Wrenn Inventoria and his most trusted lieutenants. The ancient city's defences, including magical traps and guardians, activate as the two sides clash. The party must utilise their combined strengths and knowledge to defeat Wrenn and overcome the city's challenges to reach the ultimate treasure.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
After defeating Wrenn, the party uncovers ancient knowledge and powerful artefacts, allowing Zenki Tenders to collect the Books of Lost Knowledge. They must decide how to share or protect these treasures while navigating the jungle using ancient maps and artefacts.

### Endings
1. **Triumphant Return**: The party successfully navigates back to civilisation with their newfound knowledge and artefacts. Zenki Tenders gains invaluable books, and the party is celebrated as heroes. Rewards include wealth, recognition, and powerful new items.
2. **Partial Success:** The party manages to escape with their lives, and some of the treasures, but the jungle or Wrenn's remaining forces claim some key artefacts or knowledge. They are still rewarded but with a lingering sense of what could have been.
3. **Bitter Escape**: The party is forced to flee the city with nothing but their lives after a particularly fierce battle with Wrenn's forces or the city's defences. They return with tales of the dangers and secrets of Zuthara but little to show for it, earning modest rewards for their bravery.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Acheron's Pursuit
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, stfudonny}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
The party is tasked with exploring the untamed Wildland, a dense and mysterious jungle believed to hold ancient secrets and powerful artefacts. Their journey leads them to uncover the lost history of an ancient civilisation, but they soon find themselves at odds with Keres Acheron, a reckless and pious tiefling foot soldier who seeks the same secrets for her own purposes.
### Hook
The players are independent adventurers summoned by a local historian who has discovered an ancient map leading to the Wildland. The historian offers a substantial reward for any artefacts or information the party can bring back, piquing their interest in the exploration.
### Location Descriptions
1. **The Edge of the Wildland**: A thick, overgrown border separating the known lands from the mysterious jungle. Signs of recent activity hint at other adventurers’ presence.
2. **Ruined Campsite**: The remains of a previous expedition’s camp, now destroyed and overrun by wild beasts. Clues here point to the dangers ahead.
3. **Ancient Ruins**: Crumbling structures covered in vines and moss, containing cryptic symbols and texts from a long-lost civilisation. These ruins provide vital clues to the artefact’s location.
4. **Dense Jungle Paths**: Narrow, winding trails through dense foliage, teeming with wildlife and natural obstacles. The party must navigate these paths carefully.
5. **The Ancient Temple**: A hidden temple deep within the Wildland, protected by ancient defences and containing the powerful artefact. The final confrontation with Keres takes place here.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The party arrives at the edge of the Wildland, only to find signs of another group having recently entered. As they venture deeper, they encounter remnants of this group's camp, which has been ransacked. The party must fend off a pack of wild beasts drawn by the commotion, hinting at the dangers within the jungle.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The party navigates through the dense foliage, discovering ancient ruins and remnants of a long-lost civilisation. They find cryptic symbols and texts hinting at a powerful artefact hidden deeper within the Wildland. Along the way, they encounter various wildlife and overcome natural obstacles, gathering clues about the artefact’s location.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the party deciphers the final clue to the artefact's location, they are ambushed by Keres Acheron and her followers. Keres, driven by a vision from her deity, believes that the artefact is meant for her to wield. She challenges the party, resulting in a tense standoff. The party must decide whether to negotiate, deceive, or confront Keres and her group directly.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The party arrives at the ancient temple where the artefact is hidden, only to find Keres, who is attempting to unlock its secrets. A fierce battle ensues as the party must stop Keres from obtaining the artefact. The temple's defences come to life, adding another layer of danger to the confrontation.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Keres defeated or driven off, the party retrieves the artefact and uncovers its true purpose. They return to the historian, who is amazed by their findings. The party's successful expedition marks the beginning of new explorations and discoveries, earning them fame and the promised reward.

2. **Negotiated Settlement**: The party negotiates a truce with Keres, agreeing to share the artefact’s power. They return with valuable knowledge and some of the relic’s power, earning a moderate reward and potential future alliances.
3. **Keres' Triumph**: Keres overpowers the party and takes the artefact. The players escape with their lives but empty-handed. Their reputation suffers, but they gain invaluable experience and insight into the Wildland, setting the stage for a future rematch.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Noonshade's Knitting Circle
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Chase}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
The melancholic knitter Yllana Noonshade and her diverse companions are drawn into a maze of supernatural terror in a desolate, frozen land. They must navigate an ancient labyrinth haunted by curses and monstrous guardians to confront Vexanar, a vengeful and prideful Dragonborn constable. Their quest will test their courage, wits, and bonds as they face eerie landscapes and dark secrets.
### Hook
Each player receives a mysterious letter delivered by a spectral raven. The letter invites them to the remote village of Frosthaven, promising answers to their deepest questions and fears. Intrigued and compelled by their personal motivations, they set off for the frozen land where their paths converge.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Frosthaven Village:** A desolate, snow-covered settlement where the adventure begins. Signs of struggle and an eerie silence set the stage for the mystery.
2. **Village Square:** The central gathering place, now empty and filled with an unsettling chill. It holds clues to the labyrinth's entrance.
3. **The Labyrinth Entrance:** Hidden beneath an old well, it leads into a maze of icy tunnels and supernatural dangers.
4. **Hall of Echoes:** A chamber within the labyrinth where spectral images of the villagers repeat their last moments, offering clues and chills.
5. **Chamber of Frozen Guardians:** An area guarded by ice monsters and remnants of past adventurers, providing insight into the labyrinth's history.
6. **The Grand Hall:** The final confrontation site, a vast hall of ice where the party faces Vexanar. The walls shift, and spectral villagers add to the peril.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the group arrives in Frosthaven, they are met with an ominous silence and signs of recent struggle. The villagers are missing, and an unnatural cold seeps into their bones. They soon encounter Vexanar, who cryptically warns them to leave or face the labyrinth's wrath. The party refuses, driven by the need to uncover the truth behind the village's plight.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The party explores Frosthaven, discovering clues about the ancient labyrinth beneath the ice. They learn of an ancient curse that binds the village's fate to the labyrinth and its guardian, Vexanar. The villagers' souls are trapped within and used to fuel Vexanar's dark powers. They must enter the labyrinth to save the villagers and break the curse.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Within the labyrinth, the group faces various supernatural challenges, including phantasmal echoes of the villagers and grotesque, icy monsters. They find the frozen remains of past adventurers and uncover journals that reveal Vexanar's tragic past. This constable was once the village's protector but was cursed to guard the labyrinth eternally. The labyrinth shifts and changes, escaping nearly impossible.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The party confronts Vexanar in a grand hall of ice and shadows at the labyrinth's heart. They must fight him while contending with the labyrinth's shifting walls and spectral villagers trying to hinder their progress. Vexanar, driven by vengeance and pride, proves a formidable foe, but the party's determination and unity give them the edge.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
Upon Vexanar's defeat, the curse breaks, and the labyrinth begins to collapse. The villagers' souls are freed, and the party must escape the crumbling maze. They emerge from the ice, carrying the hope of a new dawn for Frosthaven. The villagers, now freed, thank them, and each party member finds a sense of closure and purpose.

# Echoes of Pegasusflight
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Haley Pix}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
Greta Flintfinder, a sceptical yet adventurous dwarf banker, embarks on a perilous journey into an eerie, haunted jungle to break an ancient curse. Joined by Berrian Hawthorn, a greedy yet diplomatic court magician, and Daphne Falconwing, an inquisitive yet overconfident draper, Greta must navigate a labyrinth of fear and overcome supernatural challenges. Their ultimate goal is to confront Bennett Pegasusflight, a jovial yet overconfident jeweller who has become the unwilling guardian of the curse.
### Hook
The adventurers are drawn together by rumours of a cursed labyrinth hidden deep within a jungle, promising riches and powerful artefacts. Greta Flintfinder seeks to debunk the myth, the potential wealth lures Berrian Hawthorn, and Daphne Falconwing is driven by a sense of duty to break the curse.
### Location Descriptions
1. **The Jungle Entrance:** A dense, overgrown path marked by eerie silence and occasional supernatural whispers. It serves as the gateway to the labyrinth.
2. **Disorienting Paths:** The jungle's interior is filled with shifting paths and hidden dangers. Ghostly figures offer cryptic advice and warnings.
3. **The Hidden Temple:** An ancient, overgrown structure housing an enchanted compass crucial for navigating the labyrinth.
4. **The Labyrinth Entrance:** A foreboding doorway marked with ancient runes leading into the heart of the cursed maze.
5. **Illusory Chambers:** Various rooms within the labyrinth that project illusions based on the adventurers' fears and desires, testing their resolve.
6. **Heart of the Labyrinth:** The final chamber where Bennett Pegasusflight resides, filled with treasures and the source of the curse.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon entering the jungle, the group is immediately faced with disorienting paths and eerie whispers that seem to emanate from the trees themselves. The jungle appears alive, shifting and rearranging itself to confuse and trap intruders. They must find a way to navigate the treacherous terrain and uncover the entrance to the labyrinth.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
As they delve deeper, the adventurers encounter remnants of previous expeditions, including cryptic journals and maps. They meet spectral figures who offer cryptic advice and warnings, suggesting that the labyrinth's curse stems from an ancient betrayal. Clues lead them to a hidden temple, where they find an old, enchanted compass that points the way forward.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The adventurers soon discover that the labyrinth is not just a physical maze but a psychological one. Each member faces illusions tailored to their deepest fears and desires. Visions of financial ruin haunt Greta, Berrian by images of untold wealth just out of reach, and Daphne by challenges to her sense of honour. They must confront and overcome these illusions to proceed.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
At the labyrinth's heart, they find Bennett Pegasusflight, who has been driven mad by his overconfidence and the curse’s influence. He believes himself to be the labyrinth's true master. A tense confrontation ensues, where they must defeat Bennett and break the enchantments binding him to the labyrinth. This battle tests their strength, wits, and unity.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Bennett defeated, the labyrinth begins to collapse. The adventurers make a harrowing escape, emerging from the jungle just as the entrance seals itself forever. The curse is lifted, and they find themselves in possession of a few ancient treasures and a newfound respect for the supernatural.

# Cursed by the Maze
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, None}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a frozen, desolate land, a labyrinth of ancient horrors has awakened, spreading terror and supernatural phenomena across the region. Iados Voidheart, a cunning yet impulsive Tiefling squire, and his wise but secretive Half-Elf scribe companion, Carric Daydream, are drawn into a quest to confront the labyrinth’s dark power. Their journey pits them against the vengeful and pious Himo Lightfoot, a bowyer consumed by the labyrinth’s curse. As the heroes navigate the eerie maze, they uncover chilling secrets, confront twisted creatures, and ultimately face the malevolent force at the labyrinth's heart.
### Hook
The players find themselves in the snow-covered village of Frostbane, drawn by rumours of a labyrinthine terror spreading from the frozen wastes. They encounter a desperate plea for help from the villagers, who speak of an ancient, cursed labyrinth spreading madness and death. Intrigued and seeking adventure, the players agree to investigate, joining forces with Iados Voidheart and Carric Daydream to confront the dark power lurking within the maze.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Frostbane Village**: A snow-covered settlement plagued by the labyrinth’s curse, with fearful and desperate villagers seeking help.
2. **Frozen Wilderness**: The harsh, icy landscape surrounding Frostbane is filled with supernatural occurrences and ancient runes.
3. **Labyrinth Entrance**: The foreboding entryway to the cursed maze, marked by eerie carvings and a palpable sense of dread.
4. **Twisting Corridors**: The labyrinth’s ever-shifting pathways, filled with traps, illusions, and ghostly apparitions.
5. **Heart of the Labyrinth**: The dark, central chamber where the relic resides, guarded by the corrupted Himo Lightfoot and other twisted creatures.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The adventure begins with the players arriving in Frostbane, witnessing the aftermath of the labyrinth's influence: people driven mad, livestock mysteriously slain, and an overwhelming sense of dread in the air. As they gather information, they are attacked by a group of afflicted villagers, their minds twisted by the labyrinth’s dark magic. The players must defend themselves and survive the assault.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After the attack, the players learn from the villagers about the cursed labyrinth. Guided by clues and tales, they trek through the frozen wilderness, facing harsh elements and eerie, supernatural occurrences. Along the way, they discover ancient runes and cryptic messages hinting at the labyrinth’s origins and the nature of the curse. They also encounter Himo Lightfoot, a bowyer, on a quest to destroy the labyrinth, driven by a personal vendetta.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they delve deeper into the labyrinth, the players face a series of harrowing challenges: shifting walls, ghostly apparitions, and traps designed to play on their deepest fears. They uncover the tragic backstory of the labyrinth’s creation – it was once a place of worship, twisted by dark magic and betrayal. Himo’s true intentions come to light; he seeks to harness the labyrinth's power for himself, believing it will grant him divine retribution against those who wronged him.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
In the labyrinth’s heart, the players confront Himo Lightfoot, who has succumbed to the labyrinth’s corrupting influence. The confrontation escalates into a fierce battle, with Himo wielding dark powers granted by the labyrinth. The players must overcome Himo’s relentless assault, the labyrinth’s nightmarish traps, and their own fears to reach the source of the curse.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
Upon defeating Himo, the players discover the labyrinth’s core: a powerful relic responsible for the curse. They must decide whether to destroy it, risking the collapse of the entire labyrinth, or find a way to purify it, potentially ending the curse without further destruction. Their choice determines the fate of the frozen land and the villagers who depend on them.
{{watercolor12,top:80%,left:30%,width:800px,background-color:#78676d,opacity:0.5}}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Endings
1. **Destruction Ending**: If the players choose to destroy the relic, the labyrinth collapses, sealing the curse forever. The frozen land slowly recovers, but the village of Frostbane is lost. The players are hailed as heroes for their sacrifice.
2. **Purification Ending**: If the players purify the relic, the labyrinth’s dark power is neutralised, lifting the curse. The frozen land and Frostbane are saved, and the players are celebrated for their wisdom and bravery.
3. **Corruption Ending**: If the players decide to keep the relic’s power for themselves, they risk succumbing to its dark influence. The labyrinth’s curse remains, and the players face the moral consequences of their choice, possibly leading to future adventures involving their struggle with the relic’s corruption.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# Mirthful's Reckoning
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, John Rogers}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a remote mountain range, an ancient labyrinth has been uncovered within a sprawling cave system. Strange whispers and eerie lights have been reported by those who dare to venture near. Ula Mirthful, a resourceful but reckless halfling vanguard, along with her companions, Alfhild Slatefoot and Pheriannath Lunarwing, must navigate this labyrinthine horror to uncover the source of its supernatural occurrences and put an end to the curse that haunts it.
### Hook
The adventurers receive a mysterious letter from a distant village near the mountain range, pleading for help. The letter speaks of villagers disappearing near the newly uncovered labyrinth, strange whispers in the night, and ghostly apparitions. Intrigued and unable to resist the call of curiosity, Ula convinces her companions to investigate the labyrinth and solve the mystery behind the eerie events.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village Outskirts**: A remote village at the base of the mountain range. The villagers are frightened, whispering about the labyrinth and the disappearances.
2. **Cave Entrance**: A foreboding entrance to the labyrinth, surrounded by ghostly apparitions and eerie whispers.
3. **Winding Tunnels**: Narrow, twisting tunnels filled with shifting shadows and cryptic symbols. The air is thick with tension and supernatural presence.
4. **Ancient Altar**: A hidden chamber with a stone altar covered in ancient runes. Touching it triggers visions revealing the labyrinth's dark history.
5. **Heart of the Labyrinth**: The deepest part of the labyrinth, where the sorcerer's phylactery is hidden. It's guarded by the sorcerer's spirit and spectral minions.
6. **Collapsing Exit**: The final escape route as the labyrinth begins to collapse. The adventurers must navigate falling debris and shifting walls to make it out alive.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon arrival at the village, the adventurers find the villagers in a state of panic. They learn that a young villager recently vanished after entering the labyrinth. As night falls, whispers fill the air, and ghostly figures are seen at the entrance of the cave. The party must brave their fears and enter the labyrinth to rescue the villager and uncover the truth.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Inside the labyrinth, the adventurers encounter a series of eerie and supernatural phenomena: shifting shadows, ghostly apparitions, and whispers that seem to guide or mislead them. They explore winding tunnels, discovering ancient symbols and cryptic messages hinting at a curse placed on the labyrinth. They encounter villagers who had disappeared, now spectral guardians bound to the labyrinth by the curse.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the party delves deeper, they find an ancient altar with a cryptic inscription. Touching the altar triggers a vision, revealing that the labyrinth was created by a long-dead sorcerer who sought to trap the souls of intruders to fuel his dark magic. The adventurers must find the sorcerer's phylactery hidden within the labyrinth to break the curse. However, they are pursued by spectral guardians and must navigate traps and illusions designed to disorient them.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
In the heart of the labyrinth, the adventurers discover the sorcerer's phylactery, guarded by the spirit of the sorcerer himself, a formidable and malevolent entity. They must engage in a climactic battle against the sorcerer's spirit and his spectral minions. The labyrinth itself seems to fight against them, with walls shifting and traps activating as they battle.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
Upon destroying the phylactery, the curse is lifted, and the spirits of the trapped villagers are freed. The labyrinth begins to collapse, and the adventurers must make a daring escape. They return to the village as heroes, having banished the darkness and restored peace. The villagers, grateful and relieved, offer their heartfelt thanks and rewards.

2. **Partial Success**: The adventurers destroy the phylactery but at a great cost, losing one of their own or some of the freed spirits. They escape the labyrinth and are honoured by the villagers but with a sombre undertone.

# Curses of the Copperleaf
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Mario Padilla}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a remote mountain range, a labyrinthine cave system hides ancient secrets and deadly curses. Kaelira Cinderfall, a resourceful yet impulsive half-elf potter, and her stubborn, greedy elf companion, Lúthien Copperleaf, must navigate the eerie passages and face supernatural horrors to uncover the truth behind the labyrinth's dark history and lift the curse that haunts the region.
### Hook
The players receive a mysterious letter promising a rich reward for anyone who can uncover the secrets of the cursed labyrinth hidden deep within the mountains. Drawn by curiosity and the promise of wealth, they travel to the remote village at the mountain's base, where they meet Kaelira and Lúthien, who have personal stakes in uncovering the labyrinth's secrets.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village of Briar’s Hollow**: Nestled at the mountain's base, this small village is a tight-knit community plagued by fear and sorrow. The villagers are wary but hopeful that the players can lift the curse.
2. **Mountain Pass**: A treacherous and winding path leading up to the labyrinth's entrance. Along the way, players encounter ancient carvings, warnings, and remnants of past adventurers who failed to return.
3. **Labyrinth Entrance**: A grand, foreboding stone archway adorned with mystical symbols and ancient runes. It exudes an aura of dread, and ghostly whispers can be heard emanating from within.
4. **Inner Tunnels**: A maze of dark, winding corridors filled with traps, illusions, and spectral apparitions. The walls are etched with cryptic messages and clues about the labyrinth's history.
5. **Chamber of Trials**: The labyrinth's heart, where the players face their greatest fears and the labyrinth's guardian spirits. It is here that the ritual must be performed to lift the curse and escape the labyrinth's clutches.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon arrival at the village, the players learn that several villagers have gone missing after venturing into the mountains. The village elder, Eamon, a wise old human, explains that the labyrinth was once a sanctuary but has become a place of fear and despair. Eamon beseeches the players to find the missing villagers and uncover the cause of the labyrinth's corruption.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The players, guided by Kaelira and Lúthien, begin their trek up the mountain. They discover ancient carvings and ominous warnings as they near the labyrinth entrance. Inside, they encounter ghostly apparitions of past explorers, eerie whispers echoing through the tunnels, and remnants of failed expeditions. As they delve deeper, they find clues hinting at an ancient ritual gone awry and a powerful artefact hidden within the labyrinth's heart.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Just as they think they're making progress, the labyrinth's layout shifts magically, separating the party. They must navigate their way through the ever-changing maze, facing their worst fears brought to life by the labyrinth's magic. Along the way, they uncover more about the artefact and the ritual, realising that the labyrinth is not just a physical maze but a mental and spiritual trial.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Reunited at the labyrinth's core, the players find the artefact – a cursed amulet bound to the labyrinth's dark power. To break the curse, they must perform a dangerous ritual while fending off the labyrinth's guardian spirits and avoiding traps that test their resolve and unity. The ritual requires both Kaelira's resourcefulness, Lúthien's greed-driven determination, and the players' collective strength and cunning.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the ritual complete, the labyrinth begins to collapse, and the curse lifts. The players, Kaelira and Lúthien, must escape before being trapped forever. They return to the village as heroes, the missing villagers in tow, and the once ominous mountain now a place of peace.

## Lúthien Copperleaf
*Sidekick, Elf (She/Her)*
**Profession:** Gatherer::
**Personality Trait:** Greedy.::
**Flaw:** Stubborn.::
### Appearance
{{columns:2
**Body Type:** Feminine::
**Hairstyle:** Braided::
**Hair Colour:** Blonde::
**Skin Colour:** Pale::
**Eye Colour:** Gray::
}}
}}



\column
### Endings
1. **Successful Escape and Curse Lifted**: The players successfully perform the ritual, escape the collapsing labyrinth, and lift the curse. They are hailed as heroes and rewarded with gold, magical items, and the villagers' gratitude. Kaelira and Lúthien find closure and a new purpose.
2. **Partial Success**: The players manage to lift the curse but at a great cost, losing a companion or being severely injured. They escape with their lives and some treasure but are haunted by the experience. The villagers are grateful, but the players must deal with the lingering effects of the labyrinth's horrors.
3. **Failure and Tragic End**: The players fail to complete the ritual, resulting in the labyrinth collapsing entirely. They barely escape, but the curse remains, and the missing villagers are lost forever. They return to the village in shame, their only reward being a grim reminder of the labyrinth's dangers.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# The Wraith's Roar
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Mauro}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a gothic horror-themed one-shot, players are drawn into a dark, supernatural adventure aboard a haunted pirate ship, "The Wraith's Roar," captained by the vengeful Tiefling, Johnny Demon Eyes. The players must navigate a maze-like ghost ship, confront ancient curses, and ultimately face Johnny Demon Eyes himself. With the help of Moeru, a fearless and loyal Tiefling mercenary known as the Crimson Devil, they unravel the ship's dark secrets and seek to put an end to Johnny's reign of terror.
### Hook
The players, seasoned adventurers in their own right, find themselves in a coastal town haunted by tales of "The Wraith's Roar," a ghostly ship seen off the shore. They are approached by Moeru, a renowned Tiefling mercenary known as the Crimson Devil, who seeks their aid in confronting Johnny Demon Eyes, a pirate captain who has terrorised the seas and taken something precious from her. She promises rich rewards and a chance to rid the world of a great evil.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Coastal Town:** A small, fog-covered fishing village plagued by tales of ghost ships and missing sailors. The townsfolk are superstitious and wary, providing the players with initial clues about "The Wraith's Roar."
2. **The Wraith's Roar Deck:** The main deck of the ghost ship is shrouded in an eerie fog. Ghostly figures can be seen drifting in and out of the mist, and the sounds of phantom sailors echo through the air.
3. **Labyrinthine Corridors:** The twisted, maze-like passages below deck, filled with shifting walls, ghostly whispers, and traps left by the cursed crew. The players must navigate these corridors to find clues and artefacts.
4. **Captain's Quarters:** The grand yet decaying room where Johnny Demon Eyes resides. The walls are adorned with macabre trophies, and the room emanates a palpable sense of dread. This is the setting for the final confrontation.
5. **Escape Route:** The players' path to freedom as "The Wraith's Roar" begins to sink. Collapsing corridors, rising water, and the remnants of the ghostly crew create a tense and dangerous escape sequence.


::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players and Moeru set sail to investigate the sightings of "The Wraith's Roar," they are suddenly enveloped in a supernatural fog. Their ship is boarded by ghostly pirates, leading to a fierce battle. Amidst the chaos, the players are forced onto the haunted ship itself, separating them from their vessel and any hope of immediate retreat.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Aboard "The Wraith's Roar," the players explore the eerie, labyrinthine corridors of the ghost ship. They encounter spectral visions of the ship's cursed crew and find cryptic clues about Johnny Demon Eyes and the ancient curse binding the ship to the mortal plane. Moeru shares her personal vendetta against Johnny, revealing that he took her sister's eyes and left her for dead. The players must piece together the history and layout of the ship, solving puzzles and avoiding traps set by the ghostly crew.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the players delve deeper, they discover that Johnny Demon Eyes has been using powerful necromantic artefacts to maintain his control over the ship and its crew. Just as they find a way to confront Johnny, they are ambushed by a horde of vengeful spirits. During the battle, Moeru becomes possessed by a powerful wraith, complicating their mission. The players must find a way to free her without harming her, adding a layer of urgency and difficulty to their quest.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players finally reach Johnny Demon Eyes in the captain's quarters, a grand, decaying room filled with trophies from his many victims. A climactic battle ensues, with Johnny using his cunning and supernatural abilities to try and outwit the players. The haunted ship itself seems to come alive, with the walls shifting and ghostly hands reaching out to hinder the players. They must destroy the necromantic artefacts and defeat Johnny to break the curse.

#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Johnny Demon Eyes defeated and the necromantic artefacts destroyed, "The Wraith's Roar" begins to crumble and sink. The players, along with a freed Moeru, must escape the collapsing ship. As they reach the shore, the fog lifts and the ghostly presence dissipates. The town celebrates their return, and Moeru offers them her heartfelt thanks, along with a share of treasure recovered from the ship.
### Endings
1. **Triumphant Victory:** The players successfully defeat Johnny Demon Eyes, destroy the necromantic artefacts, and escape with Moeru. They are hailed as heroes, receiving both the gratitude of the town and a substantial treasure.
2. **Bittersweet Success:** The players manage to defeat Johnny but are unable to save Moeru from the wraith's possession. They escape the sinking ship with heavy hearts, earning respect but feeling the weight of their loss.
3. **Tragic Defeat:** The players fail to overcome Johnny Demon Eyes, resulting in their entrapment aboard The Wraith's Roar. They become part of the ship's cursed crew, their spirits doomed to haunt the seas for eternity.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Labyrinth of the Cursed Mountain
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Nathan N}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the Gothic Horror one-shot "Labyrinth of the Cursed Mountain," the adventurers find themselves entangled in a tale of ancient curses and haunted landscapes. Henryk the Gravetender, alongside his companions Quarion Starlight and Snagra Dragonfist, must navigate a treacherous mountain and its labyrinthine caves to confront the malevolent force responsible for a series of gruesome deaths. Deep within the mountain, they will face the twisted machinations of Jagg Frostborn, a once-trusted royal advisor turned into a harbinger of doom. Their mission is not only to end the curse but also to prevent further loss of innocent lives.
### Hook
The adventurers receive an urgent summons from a nearby village nestled at the foot of a foreboding mountain. Mysterious and gruesome deaths have been plaguing the village, and the villagers, desperate for help, have turned to anyone willing to brave the dangers of the cursed mountain. Henryk, motivated by his duty to prevent unnecessary deaths, rallies his companions Quarion and Snagra to investigate and put an end to the terror.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village of Eldenfoot**: A small, isolated village at the base of the cursed mountain, filled with fearful villagers and an eerie, oppressive atmosphere.
2. **Mountain Path**: A treacherous, winding trail through dense, twisted forests and jagged cliffs, marked by ancient runes and ghostly apparitions.
3. **Hermit's Hut**: A secluded, dilapidated hut inhabited by an old hermit who provides crucial information about the mountain’s curse and Jagg Frostborn.
4. **Labyrinth Entrance**: A dark, foreboding cave opening leading into the labyrinthine network of tunnels within the mountain.
5. **Shifting Passages**: The ever-changing tunnels of the labyrinth, filled with traps, illusions, and restless spirits, are designed to confuse and ensnare intruders.
6. **Knight's Rest**: A hidden chamber where the spirit of a former royal knight offers guidance and assistance to the adventurers.

::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the adventurers arrive at the village, they are greeted by the fearful and desperate villagers. The village elder explains that people have been found dead with expressions of pure terror etched on their faces, and whispers of an ancient curse have resurfaced. That night, a villager goes missing, and the adventurers decide to ascend the mountain in pursuit.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The ascent is treacherous, with the path winding through dense, twisted forests and jagged cliffs. Along the way, they encounter eerie signs: ghostly apparitions, unsettling whispers, and ancient runes carved into the rocks. They find clues hinting at the mountain’s dark history, including the tale of a cursed labyrinth hidden within the caves. They meet an old hermit who warns them of Jagg Frostborn, revealing that she was once a royal advisor who succumbed to greed and despair, cursing the mountain in her madness.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Deep within the labyrinthine caves, the adventurers face various trials: traps, illusions, and restless spirits. They discover that the labyrinth itself is alive, shifting and changing to confuse intruders. They also learn that Jagg Frostborn is using dark magic to manipulate the labyrinth, heightening their sense of dread and danger. An unexpected ally, the spirit of a former royal knight, offers them guidance and a way to navigate the shifting passages.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
At the heart of the labyrinth, they confront Jagg Frostborn in a grand, eerie chamber filled with ancient relics and dark energy. The battle is intense, with Jagg using her formidable dark magic and the power of the labyrinth against them. Henryk’s stoic resolve, Quarion’s confidence, and Snagra’s vengeful fury are tested to their limits as they fight to break the curse.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
Upon Jagg’s defeat, the dark magic dissipates, and the labyrinth begins to collapse. The adventurers must make a desperate escape, navigating the crumbling passages. They emerge from the mountain, victorious but weary. The curse is lifted, and the grateful villagers celebrate their heroes. Henryk reflects on the lives saved while Quarion and Snagra find solace in their shared triumph.

::
### Endings
1. **Heroic Victory**: The adventurers successfully defeat Jagg and escape the collapsing labyrinth. The villagers honour them, and they gain powerful relics from the mountain's depths. Henryk feels a sense of fulfilment, Quarion's confidence grows, and Snagra's thirst for vengeance is sated.
2. **Pyrrhic Victory**: They defeat Jagg, but the labyrinth's collapse claims one of their lives. The survivors are hailed as heroes, but they carry the weight of their loss. Henryk is further burdened by sorrow, Quarion becomes more sombre, and Snagra’s vengeance feels hollow.
3. **Tragic Defeat**: The adventurers fail to defeat Jagg or escape the labyrinth. The curse continues, and the village falls into despair. Their sacrifice becomes a cautionary tale, and the mountain remains a place of fear. The story of Henryk, Quarion, and Snagra becomes a ghostly legend whispered by those who dare to venture near the cursed mountain.{{pageNumber,auto}}
