# Secrets of the Eerie Maze
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Nihilm}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of a desolate wetland, an ancient labyrinth guards a curse that has plagued the land for centuries. Vyrith Frostfall, a charismatic Dragonborn high priestess, and her melancholic yet prideful human companion, Alice, must navigate this eerie maze to uncover the truth behind the curse. Haunted by supernatural forces and pursued by the relentless bounty hunter, Alistair Leviathansurge, they must confront their deepest fears and unravel the secrets of the labyrinth before time runs out.
### Hook
The players are drawn into the story when they receive a mysterious letter begging for help to lift the curse that has befallen the wetlands. The letter hints at a great reward and the promise of ancient knowledge. Vyrith and Alice find themselves compelled to investigate, driven by their own personal quests and the allure of solving an ancient mystery.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village Outskirts**: The starting point where Vyrith and Alice encounter fearful villagers and gather initial information about the curse.
2. **Foggy Wetlands**: A maze of treacherous paths, haunted by ghostly apparitions and filled with cryptic messages leading to the labyrinth.
3. **Hermit's Hut**: A secluded dwelling of a hermit who provides crucial information about the labyrinth and the curse's origins.
4. **Ancient Willow**: A sentient tree that offers wisdom and clues about the labyrinth’s shifting nature and the needed sacrifice.
5. **Labyrinth Entrance**: The foreboding gateway to the maze, marked by ancient symbols and guarded by restless spirits.
6. **Heart of the Labyrinth**: The submerged temple where the final confrontation with Alistair takes place, and the ritual of lifting the curse takes place.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Vyrith and Alice arrive at the edge of the wetlands, greeted by an eerie silence and an oppressive fog. As they venture deeper, they encounter villagers who speak of the labyrinth's curse, warning them of vengeful spirits and the bounty hunter Alistair, who hunts those who seek the labyrinth's secrets. The villagers' fear and desperation set the stage for the adventure.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The duo explores the wetlands, gathering clues about the labyrinth’s location and the nature of the curse. They encounter strange phenomena, such as ghostly apparitions and cryptic messages etched into trees. Conversations with a reclusive hermit and an ancient willow tree spirit reveal that the labyrinth's curse stems from a forgotten betrayal and that only a true sacrifice can lift it.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Just as they piece together the puzzle, Alistair Leviathansurge ambushes them, revealing that he has been hired to ensure the curse remains intact. He believes that the labyrinth holds a secret that must not be uncovered. A tense chase ensues through the labyrinthine wetlands, with Vyrith and Alice using their wits and magical abilities to evade capture while deciphering the labyrinth's shifting pathways.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Vyrith and Alice finally reach the heart of the labyrinth, a forgotten temple submerged in murky waters. Alistair catches up, leading to a climactic battle amidst the crumbling ruins and rising tides. The confrontation forces each character to confront their flaws and make choices that will determine the fate of the curse and their own destinies.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Alistair defeated, Vyrith and Alice perform a ritual that unveils the true nature of the curse. They must decide whether to sacrifice a precious personal item to lift the curse or to take another path that may leave the curse dormant but unchanged. Their decision affects not only the wetlands but also their own personal growth and relationship.

3. **The Curse Strengthened**: If they fail to perform the ritual correctly or make selfish choices, the curse intensifies, turning the wetlands into a more dangerous place. They escape with their lives but carry the guilt and consequences of their actions.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# The Tormented Labyrinth
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, CoreWatch}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of a vast and treacherous sea, the once-prosperous island of Ebonreach now lies shrouded in eternal twilight and chilling fog. Legends whisper of an ancient labyrinth beneath the island, cursed and haunted by the tormented souls of those who perished seeking its secrets. When an old enemy resurfaces and threatens the balance of the realm, a diverse group of adventurers must navigate the labyrinth’s dark passages to break the curse and save Ebonreach from impending doom.
### Hook
The adventurers receive a mysterious missive from Merrick Falconwing, a renowned fletcher seeking vengeance against Shorga Cragheart, a half-orc knight who has recently reappeared and claimed dominion over Ebonreach. Each party member, having their own reasons for despising Shorga, is drawn to Merrick's call. They are promised not only the chance to exact their revenge but also the opportunity to uncover the labyrinth's ancient treasures.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Ebonreach Village:** Once a bustling port, it is now a ghostly, fog-shrouded settlement with clues of its tragic past and hints to the labyrinth's location.
2. **The Labyrinth Entrance:** Hidden deep within the island’s forest, marked by ancient, weathered stones and guarded by spectral forces.
3. **The Twisted Forest:** Dense and dark, filled with eerie sounds and shadowy figures. Paths twist and turn, leading adventurers deeper into the island’s heart.
4. **Spectral Guardian’s Chamber:** A ghostly, ethereal space within the labyrinth where a spectral guardian provides vital information and moral challenges.
5. **The Crumbling Chamber:** The labyrinth's heart, an ancient and decayed grand hall where the final confrontation with Shorga Cragheart takes place.
6. **Shorga’s Stronghold:** An ominous fortress within the labyrinth, filled with dark magic, traps, and Shorga’s undead minions.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The adventurers arrive at Ebonreach’s desolate shores, where they find the once-thriving village now eerily silent. The fog-choked docks hold clues of a recent struggle. They must fend off a band of Shorga’s loyal undead warriors, revealing the island’s cursed nature and the urgent need to delve into the labyrinth.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The party explores the village, piecing together the story of the labyrinth and the curse through encounters with spectral villagers and ancient texts. They learn that Shorga seeks a powerful artefact hidden within the labyrinth, intending to use it to cement her power. The adventurers must locate the labyrinth's entrance, hidden within the island’s dense, twisted forests.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Inside the labyrinth, the adventurers face intricate traps, puzzles, and eerie illusions. They encounter a spectral guardian who reveals a crucial twist: the curse is tied to Shorga’s bloodline. To break it, they must either destroy Shorga or convince her to make amends for her ancestors' sins. Tensions rise as the party grapples with this moral dilemma amidst the labyrinth's horrors.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Deep within the labyrinth, the adventurers confront Shorga in a grand, crumbling chamber. The confrontation escalates into a fierce battle, surrounded by spectral watchers and shifting shadows. They must use their wits and might to overcome Shorga’s dark powers and her horde of cursed minions.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Shorga defeated or redeemed, the curse begins to lift. The labyrinth’s horrors recede, and the spirits trapped within are finally at peace. The island of Ebonreach slowly returns to its former state, and the adventurers emerge as heroes, forever changed by their ordeal.

### Endings

2. **Redemption and Peace:** The party convinces Shorga to atone for her ancestors' sins, lifting the curse. Shorga becomes a guardian of the island, and the adventurers receive a powerful artefact as a token of gratitude from the spirits.
3. **Pyrrhic Victory:** The adventurers defeat Shorga, but at great personal cost. The labyrinth's treasures are theirs, but the emotional and physical toll leaves a lasting impact. The island is freed, but the victory feels hollow.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# Fireheart's Ambition
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Ryan Cushman}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of a haunted forest lies an ancient labyrinth cursed by dark magic. Soren Blackwood, a stoic human sewer, accompanied by the wise yet stubborn Oda Whiskwidget and the vengeful yet loyal Uzzle Rattlebag, must navigate the labyrinth to uncover its secrets and lift the curse. Standing in their way is Irhtos Fireheart, a wise yet stubborn Dragonborn king whose inquisitive nature hides a darker ambition. The adventurers must face supernatural horrors, solve ancient riddles, and confront their deepest fears to succeed.
### Hook
The players receive a distressing letter from a nearby village describing strange occurrences in the forest. Villagers speak of a labyrinth that appears and disappears, trapping those who enter. The letter pleads for help, promising a substantial reward for anyone who can lift the curse. As independent adventurers, the players are drawn by the promise of gold and the challenge of solving the mystery.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village Edge**: A small, frightened village on the edge of a haunted forest. The villagers are desperate for help and offer guidance to the labyrinth's entrance.
2. **Labyrinth Entrance**: A shifting, mystical gateway that appears and disappears at the forest's edge. It is the first test of the players' resolve.
3. **Twisting Paths**: The ever-changing paths of the labyrinth, filled with supernatural traps, restless spirits, and ancient carvings hinting at the labyrinth's history.
4. **Ancient Altar**: The heart of the labyrinth where the dark magic is strongest. It is guarded by a powerful wraith and holds the key to lifting the curse.
5. **Labyrinth Heart**: The final chamber where the players confront Irhtos Fireheart and the powerful wraith in a climactic battle to destroy the altar and lift the curse.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon arriving at the village, the players find it in a state of panic. A villager informs them that several people have gone missing after venturing into the forest. That night, eerie whispers and ghostly apparitions lead the players to the labyrinth's entrance, which has mysteriously appeared on the forest's edge.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The labyrinth is an intricate network of twisting paths and dead ends, filled with supernatural traps and ancient carvings. As the players explore, they encounter restless spirits and uncover fragments of an old legend about a Dragonborn king who sought immortality and was cursed. Oda's piety helps them decipher religious symbols that point to the labyrinth's dark history, while Uzzle's artistic skills reveal hidden passageways through ancient murals.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players discover that the labyrinth shifts its layout, driven by dark magic. They must solve a series of riddles to stabilise the paths. Just as they make progress, they encounter Irhtos Fireheart, who reveals his connection to the labyrinth. He offers a deal: help him break the curse, and he will guide them through safely. However, it's clear that Irhtos has his own agenda, and trusting him might be risky.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Deep in the labyrinth, the players find the heart of the curse: an ancient altar guarded by a powerful wraith. Irhtos's true motives are revealed—he seeks to use the altar's power for himself. A fierce battle ensues, with the players fighting both the wraith and Irhtos. They must destroy the altar to lift the curse and prevent Irhtos from gaining its power.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the altar destroyed, the labyrinth begins to crumble. The players escape just in time, emerging from the forest to find the villagers freed from the curse. The village celebrates their heroes, offering the promised reward. The forest, once haunted, now holds only the whispers of those who once wandered its cursed paths.

### Endings

2. **Bittersweet Victory**: The players lift the curse, but Irhtos escapes, vowing revenge. The forest is freed, but a new threat looms on the horizon. The villagers reward the players, but they remain wary of future dangers.
3. **Tragic Defeat**: The players fail to destroy the altar, and Irhtos gains its power. The labyrinth's curse intensifies, and the players barely escape with their lives. The villagers mourn their losses, and the forest remains a place of darkness and fear.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Echoes of the Forgotten Civilisation
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Rockie}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
The players find themselves drawn to a dense, labyrinthine jungle filled with ancient ruins and supernatural horrors. An ancient curse has reawakened, and the jungle itself seems alive with malevolent intent. To lift the curse and escape with their lives, the players must navigate the perilous terrain, confront ghostly apparitions, and solve the mystery of the forgotten civilisation that once thrived there.
### Hook
The players each receive a mysterious letter promising untold riches and ancient secrets hidden within the heart of the jungle. The lure of adventure and the thrill of discovery compel them to embark on a journey into the unknown, where darkness and danger lurk at every turn.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Jungle's Edge:** The initial entry point into the jungle is marked by dense foliage and a sense of foreboding.
2. **Abandoned Camp:** A site of a recent struggle, providing the first clues of the danger that lies ahead.
3. **Ancient Ruins:** Crumbling structures and enigmatic carvings hint at the history of the lost civilisation.
4. **Spectral Grove:** A haunted area where players encounter ghostly apparitions offering cryptic warnings.
5. **Hidden Labyrinth Entrance:** A concealed entrance leading to the underground maze at the heart of the jungle.
6. **Twisting Passages:** The labyrinth itself is filled with traps, puzzles, and supernatural threats.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The players arrive at the jungle's edge and encounter the first signs of trouble: eerie whispers in the wind, flickering shadows in the trees, and a foreboding sense of being watched. As they delve deeper, they come across a recently abandoned camp littered with signs of a violent struggle. Claw marks, overturned tents, and bloodstains hint at the danger ahead. They must decide whether to press on or turn back, knowing that whatever happened here could happen to them.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Venturing further into the jungle, the players find remnants of an ancient civilisation—crumbling statues, overgrown temples, and enigmatic carvings. They must piece together the history of this lost society, uncovering clues about the curse that plagues the land. Along the way, they encounter spectral figures who offer cryptic warnings and fleeting glimpses of the past. These encounters provide vital information but also heighten the sense of dread and urgency.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players discover a hidden entrance to an underground labyrinth, the heart of the jungle's dark secret. As they navigate the twisting passages, they face a series of harrowing challenges: traps, puzzles, and the ever-present threat of supernatural entities. When they think they've found a way forward, the ground collapses, separating them and forcing them to confront their fears alone. They must find a way to reunite and continue their quest.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The cursed artefact in the labyrinth is guarded by a powerful spirit. Players must confront this malevolent force using their wits, courage, and knowledge. The spirit manipulates their fears, and they must work together to lift the curse and escape the labyrinth.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the curse lifted and the artefact destroyed, the jungle reclaims its natural state. The oppressive darkness lifts, and the spirits of the ancient civilisation find peace. The players emerge from the jungle, forever changed by their ordeal. They carry with them the knowledge and treasures they have acquired and a newfound appreciation for the fragile boundary between the living and the supernatural.
{{watercolor8,top:60%,left:40%,width:500px,background-color:#78676d,opacity:0.4}}
{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page


{{imageMaskEdge7,--offset:0%,--rotation:0
  ![](https://cdn.midjourney.com/a61523d7-25bf-4817-bf66-d55f271e7618/0_3.png){height:100%,bottom:-10%}
}}




### Endings
1. **Triumphant Return:** The players successfully lift the curse and escape the jungle. They return as heroes, bringing ancient relics and tales of their bravery. Their exploits earn them fame and fortune.
2. **Pyrrhic Victory:** The players, at great personal cost and sacrifice, manage to lift the curse. Some may have been lost or severely injured, and the experience leaves them haunted by the horrors they faced. They return with treasures but also with scars that will never heal, a constant reminder of the price they paid.
3. **Tragic Failure:** The players cannot lift the curse and are consumed by the jungle's malevolent forces. Their disappearance becomes yet another legend, a warning to future adventurers who might dare to venture into the haunted wilderness.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# The Cursed Maze of Ulmarsh
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, HailedZebra}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the haunted wetlands of Ulmarsh, a labyrinthine maze conceals an ancient curse that has begun to affect the nearby villages. The Dragonborn sculptor Ulvir Plainswalker, driven by his greed for a mysterious artefact, is joined by Whurdred Cragjaw, a dwarf necromancer, and Ronan Goldleaf, a stoic innkeeper, to navigate the eerie labyrinth. They must confront the vengeful bowyer Jarnk Stormgrin, who guards the heart of the curse with cunning traps and dark magic.
### Hook
The players are drawn to Ulmarsh by rumours of a powerful artefact hidden within a cursed maze. Each has their own reason: Ulvir seeks the artefact for wealth, Whurdred aims to lift the curse, and Ronan is driven by mysterious motivations. They meet at the edge of the wetlands, forming a reluctant alliance to brave the horrors within.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Ulmarsh Wetlands**: A vast, eerie landscape filled with dense fog, twisted trees, and murky waters. Ghostly apparitions and dangerous wildlife create an atmosphere of constant dread.
2. **Maze Entrance**: Marked by ancient runes and carvings, this area serves as the threshold to the labyrinth. The entrance is hidden by thick fog and guarded by will-o'-wisps.
3. **Shifting Passages**: Within the maze, the paths change frequently, filled with traps, puzzles, and spectral guardians. The walls are lined with arcane symbols that shift and move, creating a disorienting effect.
4. **Hidden Chamber**: A secret room containing a fragment of the artefact and important clues about the curse. It is protected by complex puzzles and powerful illusions.
5. **Heart of the Maze**: The final confrontation area where Jarnk Stormgrin resides. It is heavily guarded by traps and dark enchantments, with the artefact at its centre. The environment here is oppressive and filled with the aura of ancient magic.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the party enters the Ulmarsh wetlands, they are immediately met with resistance from the swamp itself. Strange, ghostly apparitions appear, attacking the party and forcing them to navigate through the treacherous terrain while avoiding quicksand and aggressive wildlife. Their initial goal is to reach the entrance of the labyrinth, which is obscured by dense fog and eerie will-o'-wisps.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Upon finding the entrance, the players discover ancient runes and carvings hinting at the history of the curse. They encounter various traps and puzzles within the maze, as well as spectral entities and undead guardians raised by the maze’s ancient magic. Along the way, they find clues about Jarnk Stormgrin’s involvement and his connection to the artefact. The maze's shifting walls and magical darkness challenge their navigation skills, requiring them to work together.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they delve deeper, the maze begins to play tricks on their minds, creating illusions and manifesting their deepest fears. The players find a hidden chamber containing a fragment of the artefact, which reveals that the curse is tied to Jarnk's vengeful spirit. At this point, the maze's magic intensifies, causing the paths to change more frequently and trapping the party in a loop unless they can solve a particularly complex puzzle that tests their trust and teamwork.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The party faces Jarnk Stormgrin, a half-orc bowyer using physical traps and dark enchantments to hinder progress. The climax involves combat and dismantling Jarnk's protective wards around an artefact. The party must use their wits and skills to break through these defenses and defeat Jarnk, whose vengeful nature drives him to fight fiercely.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Jarnk defeated, the curse begins to lift. The artefact is now within the party’s grasp, and they must decide what to do with it. Whurdred might want to destroy it to prevent further misuse, while Ulvir sees an opportunity for immense profit. Ronan's mysterious motives are revealed, providing a personal twist to the resolution. The party’s decisions here will determine the ultimate fate of the wetlands and their inhabitants.

2. **Power Ending**: Ulvir claims the artefact for himself, seeking wealth and power. This choice may lead to further consequences, including potential corruption or new enemies. The wetlands remain haunted, but the party leaves with their prize.
3. **Compromise Ending**: The party finds a middle ground, perhaps hiding the artefact or using it for the greater good. This ending brings a nuanced resolution, with potential future adventures hinted at by unresolved elements of the curse and the artefact’s power.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# The Cursed Labyrinth of Kerton
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Shaun B}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In this Gothic horror one-shot, players are drawn into a tale of revenge, supernatural curses, and labyrinthine fear set on the eerie riverbank estate of Jedediah Kerton. Haunted by the vengeful spirit of Felicia Dunstade, the former landowner, the players must navigate a treacherous maze of horrors to uncover the truth and break the curse plaguing the land.
### Hook
The players, drawn by rumours of a cursed estate and the promise of a handsome reward, arrive at the riverbank home of Jedediah Kerton. A melancholic landowner and former cleric, Jedediah seeks their help in vanquishing the vengeful spirit of Felicia Dunstade, who has made the labyrinthine forest near his estate her haunting ground.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Jedediah’s Estate**: A grand yet decaying manor by the riverbank, surrounded by overgrown gardens and dark woods. The air is thick with melancholy and the whispers of the past.
2. **Labyrinthine Forest**: A twisted, maze-like forest that shifts and changes, filled with ghostly apparitions, cryptic symbols, and eerie sounds.
3. **Hidden Chapel**: An ancient, abandoned chapel dedicated to a forgotten deity, filled with relics and forbidden rituals that hint at Felicia’s tragic past and her betrayal.
4. **Ancient Clearing**: The heart of the labyrinth, a hauntingly beautiful clearing where Felicia’s spirit lingers, surrounded by remnants of her life and death. The final confrontation takes place here.


\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players arrive at the estate, they witness supernatural occurrences and hear tales of Felicia’s ghost tormenting the locals. Jedediah explains his personal connection to Felicia, revealing her vengeful spirit is a result of an ancient curse tied to his family. The players must decide whether to help him by entering the cursed labyrinth to find and confront Felicia's spirit.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The players venture into the labyrinthine forest, a twisted maze of paths and hidden dangers. Along the way, they encounter spectral apparitions, cryptic symbols, and eerie sounds that hint at Felicia's tragic past. They must piece together clues from these encounters to understand the nature of the curse and find a way to navigate deeper into the maze.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Midway through their journey, the players stumble upon an ancient, hidden chapel dedicated to a forgotten deity. Here, they discover that Felicia was not only wronged by Jedediah’s ancestors but also betrayed by the deity she once served. The players face the moral dilemma of using the deity’s forbidden rituals to weaken Felicia’s spirit or seeking an alternative, potentially more dangerous path.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
At the heart of the labyrinth, the players find Felicia's ghostly form in an ancient clearing, surrounded by the remnants of her life and death. The confrontation is intense and challenging, requiring both combat prowess and quick thinking to address Felicia’s cunning tactics. The players must decide whether to destroy her spirit or offer her peace by rectifying the ancient wrongs done to her.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Felicia’s spirit either vanquished or appeased, the curse over the estate begins to lift. Jedediah, witnessing the resolution of the curse, offers his heartfelt thanks and the promised reward. The players leave the estate, forever marked by their encounter with the labyrinth and its haunting past.

# Curses of the Twisted Paths
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, João Pamboukian}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
Jaxira Rockshell, a Dragonborn jeweller, and her sidekick, Theresa Highpeak, a charismatic human sailmaker, find themselves drawn into a labyrinthine nightmare along a haunted riverbank. This gothic horror adventure revolves around eerie supernatural elements, ancient curses, and haunted landscapes. The players must navigate the twisting paths and confront their fears to uncover the secrets buried within the labyrinth.
### Hook
The players receive a distressing letter from a fellow jeweller and friend who claims to have discovered an ancient and valuable gemstone along the riverbank. However, the friend mentions a curse tied to the stone and their growing fear of something sinister stalking them. Concerned for their friend's safety, Jaxira and Theresa decide to investigate the riverbank and unravel the mystery.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Riverbank Campsite**: A small, deserted campsite with signs of a struggle. Personal belongings scattered around, a smouldering fire, and the cryptic blood-stained cloth provide the first clues.
2. **Labyrinth Entrance**: Hidden by thick undergrowth, the entrance is marked by ancient carvings and symbols. The air is thick with an unnatural chill, and ghostly whispers echo from within.
3. **Twisting Corridors**: Dark and narrow passages filled with ancient carvings, traps, and illusions designed to disorient and frighten intruders. Shadows seem to move independently, and eerie sounds echo through the halls.
4. **Spectral Guardians' Chamber**: A larger chamber where spectral figures challenge the players with riddles and tests of courage. The walls are lined with murals depicting the history of the labyrinth and its curse.
5. **Heart of the Labyrinth**: The final chamber, where the cursed gemstone is kept. The tormented spirit of the ancient sorcerer resides here, manifesting as a powerful spectral adversary. The room is filled with magical energy, and the air is thick with tension and fear.


::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
Upon arriving at the riverbank, the players find their friend's campsite abandoned and in disarray. Signs of a struggle are evident, and the friend is nowhere to be found. The only clue is a blood-stained piece of cloth with a cryptic message hinting at a hidden labyrinth. Strange whispers and ghostly apparitions begin to appear, urging the players to leave.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Exploring the riverbank, the players encounter eerie phenomena: shadows that move on their own, ghostly figures glimpsed out of the corner of their eyes, and unsettling sounds. They discover the entrance to the labyrinth, an ancient structure hidden by thick undergrowth and nearly swallowed by the river. Inside, the labyrinth is a twisting maze of dark corridors filled with ancient carvings and ominous symbols.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the players delve deeper into the labyrinth, they find evidence of past explorers who have met grim fates. They encounter spectral guardians who test their resolve and knowledge of the labyrinth's history. The twist comes when they discover that the labyrinth itself is a sentient entity, feeding off their fear and manipulating the environment to trap them. The players must stay calm and use their wits to navigate the ever-changing maze.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players find a cursed gemstone in a labyrinth, guarded by an ancient sorcerer's spirit. To free the sorcerer's spirit, they must confront their fears and defeat the sorcerer's powerful spectral form, overcoming the labyrinth's barriers.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
After defeating the sorcerer, the labyrinth collapses, forcing players to escape with a gemstone. They emerge from the ruins, finding peace as past victims' spirits return to their natural state, allowing players to reflect on their harrowing experience.
{{watercolor11,top:70%,left:50%,width:500px,background-color:#78676d,opacity:0.5}}

### Endings
1. **Successful Escape and Gem Recovery**: The players escape with the gemstone, now cleansed of its curse. Jaxira can use it to create a powerful jewel, gaining fame and fortune. Theresa's bravery and quick thinking are praised, strengthening their bond.
2. **Partial Success, Partial Curse**: The players escape, but the gemstone retains a part of its curse. It brings both fortune and occasional misfortune, keeping Jaxira and Theresa on their toes as they navigate their newfound wealth and its supernatural consequences.
3. **Failed Escape, Labyrinth Collapses**: The players fail to escape in time, trapped within the collapsing labyrinth. Their fate remains a mystery, and the riverbank continues to be a place of eerie legends and whispered fears.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# The Weave of Midnight Fears
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Labyrinthine Fear
}}
::

{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a land where the veil between the living and the dead is thin, a cursed hill surrounded by a labyrinthine field traps all who dare approach. When rumours of this haunted place reach the ears of adventurers, curiosity and the promise of unravelling an ancient mystery draw them in. But within the twisted paths of the labyrinth, they will encounter an ancient evil, the spectral warriors of the past, and a cunning elven strategist named Mithrellas Hawkflight. The adventurers must rely on their wits and their strength to escape the cursed land and end the malevolent force before it consumes them.
### Hook
The players, each with their own reason for travelling through the region, meet at a small, weathered inn on the outskirts of a remote village. While sharing stories over a drink, they hear tales of a mysterious hill surrounded by fields that none dare approach. According to the innkeeper, those who venture too close are never seen again, lost in a labyrinthine maze of fog and shadow. A sense of curiosity—or perhaps the lure of forgotten treasures—compels the adventurers to investigate despite the locals' warnings. As a thick fog rolls in, they find themselves drawn toward the hill, with their only path forward leading into the labyrinth's heart.
### Location Descriptions
1. **The Inn:** A small, weathered building on the outskirts of the village, where the players hear the tales of the haunted hill. It’s a cosy but eerie place, with walls lined with old maps and relics from travellers who never returned.
2. **The Hill:** A desolate, windswept rise surrounded by fields. The air is thick with tension, and the fog seems to be alive, shifting and moving with a mind of its own. The hill is the entrance to the labyrinth.
3. **The Labyrinthine Fields:** A maze of twisting paths, overgrown with brambles and thorns, filled with the remnants of ancient battles. The fog here is dense and disorienting, and the sounds of distant voices and battles fill the air.
4. **The Engraved Stones:** Markers at the edge of the cursed land, each one inscribed with warnings or fragments of the labyrinth’s history. They offer clues to the players but are also traps designed to mislead the unwary.
5. **The Hidden Glade:** A clearing at the centre of the labyrinth, where the fog lifts slightly to reveal an ancient altar. This is where the players meet Mithrellas Hawkflight and where the challenge is issued.
6. **The Heart of the Labyrinth:** The final confrontation occurs here, in a space that constantly shifts and changes under Mithrellas’s control. It’s a nightmarish landscape of shifting shadows, spectral figures, and treacherous ground.

::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players approach the hill, the air grows cold, and a dense fog envelops the landscape, cutting off all routes of escape. The path behind them disappears, leaving only a narrow, winding trail leading deeper into the maze-like fields. The fog is alive with whispers and fleeting shadows, disorienting the group and filling them with a creeping sense of dread. Suddenly, they are ambushed by spectral warriors—phantoms of ancient soldiers who once fought on this very hill. The players must defend themselves from the relentless attacks while grappling with the realisation that there may be no way out.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Having survived the initial attack, the players begin to explore the labyrinthine fields. As they delve deeper, they discover remnants of ancient battles—a broken sword, a tattered banner, and the scattered bones of the fallen. Each clue they find hints at a deeper, darker history tied to the hill. They encounter strange, half-visible figures who speak in riddles or cryptic warnings, hinting at the presence of a powerful force that controls the labyrinth. As they explore further, they find a series of engraved stones, each marking the edge of the cursed land and offering fragmented insights into the labyrinth's creation. They also uncover signs of someone else navigating the labyrinth: remnants of a campfire, scattered notes, and hastily drawn maps.

**Flaw:** Overconfident.::

## Nyssa Dusksinger
*Sidekick, Half-Elf (She/Her)*
**Profession:** Brewer::
**Personality Trait:** Diplomatic. Pious.::
**Flaw:** Curious.::
### Appearance
{{columns:2
**Body Type:** Feminine::
**Hairstyle:** Curly::
**Hair Colour:** Pink::
**Skin Colour:** Very pale::
**Eye Colour:** Pink::
}}
}}

#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players stumble upon a hidden glade at the labyrinth's centre, where the fog lifts slightly, revealing an ancient, overgrown altar. As they investigate, they are confronted by Mithrellas Hawkflight, a ghostly elven strategist who reveals herself as the labyrinth architect. Mithrellas, trapped between life and death, seeks to test her strategic prowess against worthy opponents. She challenges the players: if they can outmanoeuvre her within the labyrinth, she will lift the curse and allow them to leave. However, they will become part of the labyrinth forever if they fail. The players must now navigate the shifting paths, avoid traps, and outthink Mithrellas as she manipulates the maze to her advantage.

#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
As the players near the labyrinth's heart, they face their final challenge—a confrontation with Mithrellas herself. She manifests as a powerful, spectral figure, wielding physical and magical attacks. The players must use everything they've learned about the labyrinth and its secrets to defeat her. The battlefield shifts and changes, altering the ground beneath them at Mithrellas's will. The confrontation is not just a test of strength but of strategy, as the players must find a way to disrupt Mithrellas's control over the labyrinth to weaken her.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
Upon Mithrellas's defeat, the labyrinth begins to crumble, the fog dissipating as the cursed land is freed from her control. The players find themselves back at the edge of the hill, the once-haunted fields now peaceful and still. As the sun rises, they see the land for what it truly is—a place of beauty long overshadowed by the strategist's curse. The players may now choose to continue their journey, carrying with them the knowledge and treasures they found within the labyrinth, or stay to ensure the land remains free from future curses.
### Endings
1. **Victory and Freedom:** If the players successfully outmanoeuvre and defeat Mithrellas, they lift the curse, freeing the land and all the souls trapped within the labyrinth. The players are rewarded with a treasure from Mithrellas’s hoard, as well as the gratitude of the spirits freed by their actions.
2. **Partial Success:** If the players manage to escape the labyrinth but do not defeat Mithrellas, they survive but leave the curse intact. The land remains haunted, and they may be haunted by the spectres they encountered. They receive a lesser reward, possibly a cursed item or a relic from the labyrinth.
3. **Failure and Entrapment:** If the players fail to outthink Mithrellas and are defeated, they become part of the labyrinth themselves, their souls trapped alongside the countless others who failed before them. The one-shot ends with the players' characters becoming part of the tragic history of the haunted hill.

# The Lost City of Zathar
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Bear}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
Argha Grimscar, a half-orc jeweller, embarks on an expedition to uncover the mysteries of the fabled Lost City of Zathar, hidden deep within an uncharted jungle. With her brave yet curious sidekick, Oriana Wintermoon, they face the cunning and vengeful taxer, Ogol Quickfist, who seeks the city's treasures for his dark purposes.
### Hook
The players, independent adventurers seeking fortune and glory, receive an urgent plea from Argha Grimscar. She promises great rewards for those who join her in the search for the Lost City of Zathar, rumoured to hold untold riches and ancient secrets.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Dense Jungle Entrance**: A tangled mass of vines and towering trees marking the start of the arduous journey.
2. **Ancient Markers**: Stone obelisks covered in glyphs provide clues to the city's location and ancient history.
3. **Enigmatic Forest Glade**: Home to mystical forest spirits who offer wisdom and cryptic guidance to the adventurers.
4. **Secretive Tribe Village**: Hidden within the jungle, this village is inhabited by descendants of Zathar, protectors of the city’s secrets and magic.
5. **Heart of Zathar**: The central area of the Lost City, filled with ancient ruins, powerful artefacts, and the scene of the climactic showdown.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The journey begins as Argha and Oriana, accompanied by the players, venture into the dense jungle. They soon find themselves ambushed by Ogol Quickfist's band of mercenaries, intent on stopping them. A skirmish ensues, testing the group's resolve and unity.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Surviving the ambush, the party pushes deeper into the jungle. They encounter ancient markers and strange glyphs hinting at the city’s location. Along the way, they meet enigmatic forest spirits and uncover clues that reveal the history and culture of Zathar.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they close in on the city's location, the group discovers that Zathar is not abandoned but inhabited by a secretive tribe guarding its mysteries. These inhabitants possess powerful, ancient magic, and the players must navigate delicate negotiations and avoid triggering ancient defences.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Ogol Quickfist reappears, having followed the group. He now commands a more significant force and demands the city's treasures. The final confrontation unfolds in the heart of Zathar, where players must defend the city’s secrets from Ogol's grasp, facing both his forces and the ancient magic of Zathar itself.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Ogol defeated and the city’s secrets safeguarded, the players can choose to help the tribe protect Zathar or leave with their newfound knowledge and treasures. Their decisions shape the future of the Lost City and its guardians.

### Endings
1. **Triumphant Guardians**: The players ally with the tribe, ensuring the protection and secrecy of Zathar. They are rewarded with unique artefacts and the tribe's eternal gratitude.
2. **Treasures Revealed**: Opting to take treasures from Zathar, the players leave the city with riches and powerful relics but disrupt the balance of the tribe's way of life.
3. **Dark Victory**: Should Ogol prevail, the players must escape with their lives, witnessing Zathar's secrets exploited and its magic falling into the wrong hands.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
![title_box image](https://s8.uupload.ir/files/edit_type_layer_3.png_jq0p.jpg){position:absolute,bottom:-22%,left:-3%,height:120%,mix-blend-mode:multiply,opacity:1}

# The Blightwell Confrontation
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Doug Rector}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the shadow of a desolate mountain, a village suffers under a dark curse that raises the dead from their graves. Lydia Chimeraclaw, a melancholic and pious knight, along with her brave and stoic tiefling sidekick, Felaia Blightwell, must confront the sinister forces behind the undead uprising. The journey leads them through haunted landscapes, eerie caves, and ancient curses as they uncover the truth and attempt to end the terror plaguing the land.
### Hook
The players are drawn to the village of Grimstone, nestled at the foot of the Cursed Peak, by the promise of a substantial reward offered by the villagers. Ula Overhill, a local groom known for her honour and impulsiveness, beseeches them to investigate the source of the undead that have been terrorising the village and disrupting the peace.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Grimstone Village:** A small, eerie village at the base of the Cursed Peak, plagued by undead and shadowed by a dark curse. The villagers are desperate and fearful but hopeful for salvation.
2. **Village Graveyard:** The source of the initial undead attack. Ancient and overgrown, it holds many secrets and clues about the curse's origins.
3. **Elder’s House:** The home of the village elder, filled with old tomes and relics that provide crucial information about the Cursed Peak and its dark history.
4. **Mountain Path:** A treacherous trail leading up the Cursed Peak, dotted with remnants of past adventurers and signs of ancient rituals.
5. **Cave Entrance:** The foreboding entrance to the cave system within the mountain, marked by ominous symbols and warnings.
6. **Forgotten Shrine:** Deep within the cave, this ancient shrine is the focal point of the curse, guarded by traps, puzzles, and the sorcerer's vengeful spirit.
\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players arrive in Grimstone, they are immediately beset by a wave of undead rising from the village graveyard. The villagers, including Ula, fight valiantly but are overwhelmed. Lydia, Felaia, and the players must fend off the undead to protect the villagers and secure the town.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After the battle, Ula leads the players to the village elder, who explains the history of the Cursed Peak and an ancient prophecy that foretold the return of the undead. Clues point towards a forgotten shrine deep within a cave system in the mountain, which is said to house the source of the curse. The players must explore the village and gather information, discovering hidden passages and ancient texts that guide them towards the cave.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
Inside the cave, the players encounter not only the undead but also ancient traps and puzzles protecting the shrine. As they navigate the cave's depths, they discover that the source of the curse is a powerful artefact linked to an old and tragic tale of love and betrayal. However, the artefact is guarded by the vengeful spirit of a long-dead sorcerer who complicates their progress with illusions and deadly traps.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Reaching the heart of the cave, the players confront the sorcerer's spirit in a climactic battle. To break the curse, they must destroy the artefact while fending off waves of undead and the sorcerer's powerful magic. Lydia's overconfidence and Felaia's curiosity may either aid or hinder their efforts, depending on the players' actions and decisions.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the artefact destroyed and the sorcerer's spirit vanquished, the curse is lifted, and the undead return to their rest. The players return to Grimstone as heroes, bringing peace to the village. The grateful villagers, led by Ula, reward them handsomely and offer their eternal gratitude.
{{watercolor2,top:20%,left:70%,width:500px,background-color:#78676d,opacity:0.4}}

### Endings
1. **Triumphant Victory:** The players successfully destroy the artifact and defeat the sorcerer's spirit. The village is saved, and the players are celebrated as heroes, receiving a substantial monetary reward and a rare magical item as a token of gratitude.
2. **Pyrrhic Victory:** The players destroy the artefact but at great cost, possibly losing one of their own or causing significant damage to the village. The curse is lifted, but the celebration is sombre. The players receive their reward, but the emotional toll is heavy.
3. **Tragic Defeat:** The players fail to destroy the artefact or fall to the sorcerer's traps and undead minions. The village of Grimstone is overrun by the undead, and the players' failure haunts them, leaving a dark mark on their legacy.{{pageNumber,auto}}

:::::
# The Necromancer's Curse
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Guillaume M.}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of an ancient forest, an undead uprising threatens to engulf the nearby villages. Hlin Stonehammer, a cunning and merciful dwarf bandit leader, is compelled to lead a diverse group of allies to uncover the source of the necromantic disturbance. Amidst haunted landscapes and eerie encounters, the party must confront old curses and unravel the sinister plans of a reckless dragonborn furrier, Jarex Silvershield.
### Hook
The players, independent adventurers, each with their own motivations, hear rumours of the dead rising in the Whispering Woods. As the undead menace spreads, they cross paths with Hlin Stonehammer and their group, who are determined to investigate and stop the threat.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Edge of the Whispering Woods**: The entrance to the forest is marked by thick, twisted trees and an oppressive atmosphere. Here, the party first encounters the undead.
2. **Village Outskirts**: A small, beleaguered settlement near the forest, where frightened villagers seek help against the undead threat.
3. **Eerie Trails**: Narrow, winding paths through the forest, filled with haunting sounds and strange sightings, leading deeper into danger.
4. **Hidden Glade**: A secluded, eerie clearing where the party discovers clues about Jarex's activities and faces an ambush.
5. **Ancient Temple**: The final location, a crumbling structure overrun with dark magic, where the climactic battle against Jarex takes place.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The players arrive at the edge of the Whispering Woods, drawn by reports of undead attacks on nearby villages. They encounter a group of frightened villagers fending off a small horde of zombies. As they join the battle, Hlin Stonehammer and their companions—Lavender Quicksilver, Jeremiah Ochreleaf, and Wren Sprocketpocket—arrive to assist.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After the battle, the villagers plead for help, mentioning that the undead seem to originate from deeper within the forest. The party agrees to investigate, following eerie trails and encountering strange phenomena. Along the way, they discover ancient runes hinting at a long-forgotten curse tied to the forest's history. Conversations with ghostly apparitions and deciphering cryptic messages provide further clues about the nature of the uprising.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they delve deeper, the party finds a hidden glade where they come upon a ritual site used by Jarex Silvershield. They learn that Jarex, once a loyal furrier to the villagers, has been driven mad by a cursed artefact he uncovered, believing it will grant him power over life and death. Before they can act, Jarex's minions ambush them, leading to a tense and challenging skirmish.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Following the trail to a long-abandoned temple, the party confronts Jarex in the midst of performing a dark ritual to raise an army of undead. The confrontation is intense, with Jarex summoning powerful undead creatures to aid him. The party must use their wits and teamwork to disrupt the ritual and defeat Jarex, overcoming both his recklessness and their own inner flaws.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Jarex defeated, the curse begins to lift, and the undead forces dissipate. The artefact is destroyed or secured, ensuring it can no longer be used for evil purposes. The villagers are saved, and the Whispering Woods begin to return to their natural state. The party members reflect on their journey and the bonds they've formed, each gaining a deeper understanding of their own strengths and weaknesses.

### Endings
1. **Triumphant Victory**: The party successfully defeats Jarex and destroys the cursed artefact. They are hailed as heroes by the villagers and rewarded with gold, rare items, and heartfelt gratitude. Each member gains a unique boon reflecting their growth.
2. **Pyrrhic Victory**: The party manages to stop Jarex, but at a great cost. One or more members may be gravely injured, and the forest remains somewhat tainted. The villagers are grateful but mournful, offering modest rewards. The party gains a deeper sense of the consequences of their actions.
3. **Bitter Defeat**: Jarex escapes or completes part of his ritual, leaving the forest and nearby villages in a precarious state. The party retreats to recover and plan their next move. They gain a sense of urgency and determination to prevent further catastrophe, setting the stage for future adventures.{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page

# The Demon Lord's Return
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Jack Neller}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the shadowy wetlands of Blackwater Marsh, an undead uprising threatens to engulf the land. Anna, an elite priestess of Kelemvor, and her halfling rogue companion Tricki must confront the source of the dark magic and put an end to the horror. As they navigate the haunted landscape, they face their former friend turned nemesis, Ryder, who now serves as a demon lord. This Gothic horror one-shot is filled with eerie encounters, ancient curses, and supernatural challenges.
### Hook
The players, as adventurers in the vicinity of Blackwater Marsh, hear rumours of the dead rising from their graves and attacking nearby villages. Desperate for help, the local villagers seek anyone brave enough to venture into the marsh and discover the source of the dark magic. Anna, driven by her faith, and Tricki, lured by the promise of treasure, take up the quest. They soon learn that their old friend Ryder, now a necromancer, is behind the disturbances.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Village Outskirts**: The edge of Blackwater Marsh where villagers seek refuge from the undead. Simple wooden huts and panicked inhabitants set the scene.
2. **Haunted Wetlands**: A labyrinth of murky waters, twisted trees, and thick fog. Ghostly apparitions and dangerous wildlife lurk in the shadows.
3. **Ancient Burial Ground**: The heart of the marsh, filled with old, crumbling tombstones and crypts. Necromantic symbols and corrupted altars hint at dark rituals.
4. **Ryder's Hidden Cave**: A dark, damp cavern serving as Ryder’s lair. Filled with cultists, necromantic artefacts, and unfinished rituals.
5. **Cursed Epicentre**: The central area of the marsh where Ryder performs his final ritual. Powerful undead guardians and dark magic permeate the surroundings.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As Anna and Tricki arrive at the edge of Blackwater Marsh, they encounter a group of terrified villagers fleeing from an attack. The villagers explain that undead creatures have been emerging from the swamp and attacking anyone who ventures too close. The players must fend off a group of zombies to protect the villagers and earn their trust.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After the initial battle, the villagers tell Anna and Tricki about an ancient burial ground deep within the marsh that has been disturbed. They also mention sightings of a hooded figure performing dark rituals. The players navigate through the eerie wetlands, encountering ghostly apparitions, dangerous wildlife, and remnants of past tragedies. Along the way, they find clues pointing to Ryder's involvement, such as necromantic symbols and corrupted altars.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they delve deeper into the marsh, the players discover a hidden cave that serves as Ryder's lair. Inside, they find a group of cultists performing a ritual to summon more undead. They must interrupt the ritual and defeat the cultists. However, as they do so, they learn that Ryder has already set his final plan into motion—an ancient curse that will raise an army of undead from the entire marsh.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players track Ryder to the heart of the marsh, where he is attempting to complete his ritual at the ancient burial ground. The area is filled with powerful undead guardians and dark magic. Anna and Tricki must confront Ryder, who reveals his twisted motivations and challenges them with both his necromantic powers and summoned undead. The battle is intense, requiring both combat prowess and clever strategy to overcome Ryder and his minions.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Ryder defeated, Anna and Tricki must perform a cleansing ritual to lift the curse and put the undead to rest. As the dark magic dissipates, the marsh begins to return to its natural state, and the spirits of the dead find peace. The villagers, grateful for their salvation, offer the players rewards and share stories of their bravery.

### Endings
1. **Successful Cleansing**: Anna and Tricki successfully lift the curse, and the undead are put to rest. The villagers reward them with gold, rare relics, and the gratitude of the region. Anna feels a sense of spiritual fulfilment, while Tricki enjoys the treasure and recognition.
2. **Partial Victory**: Ryder is defeated, but the cleansing ritual is only partially successful. Some undead remain, and the marsh is still dangerous. The players receive a lesser reward and are asked to return in the future to fully cleanse the area. Anna is determined to return, while Tricki weighs the danger against potential future treasures.
3. **Failure**: Ryder’s plan succeeds in part, and the undead horde overwhelms the players. They escape with their lives, but the marsh remains cursed, and the threat of the undead persists. They receive no reward, and their reputations suffer. Anna feels a deep sense of failure, while Tricki considers the personal cost of their recklessness.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Awakening the Frostwail Dead
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Aj Rife}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the desolate, frozen land of Frostwail, an ancient curse has stirred the dead from their icy slumber. Varda Summerstar, a mysterious and stoic elf, along with their eclectic companions, must uncover the truth behind the undead uprising. As they venture through haunted landscapes and confront sinister forces, they must stop the malevolent tiefling Valdimar from unleashing an ancient horror upon the world.
### Hook
The adventurers are drawn to Frostwail by a haunting vision shared among them—a vision of the dead rising and a sinister figure at the heart of it all. As they arrive in the frozen land, they encounter a village under siege by the undead, prompting them to intervene and seek answers.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Frostholm Village**: A small, snow-covered village under siege by undead. The villagers are hardy and resourceful, led by the elder Bruni.
2. **Iceclaw Mountains**: A treacherous range of icy peaks and hidden valleys, home to the ancient crypt of the sorcerer.
3. **Forgotten Crypt**: An ancient, eerie tomb filled with traps and restless spirits. The crypt holds the secrets of the sorcerer's dark pact.
4. **Ruined Fortress**: An ancient stronghold buried in the heart of the frozen wasteland. Valdimar's final hideout and the site of the climactic battle.
5. **Frozen Wasteland**: The harsh, desolate landscape surrounding Frostholm, dotted with eerie landmarks and the remnants of long-forgotten battles.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The party arrives in the village of Frostholm, where the townsfolk are desperately defending against an onslaught of undead creatures. Varda, Dathlue, Fyrn, and Kumquat must fend off the initial wave of undead and save as many villagers as possible. In the aftermath, the village elder, a wizened dwarf named Bruni, pleads for their help to uncover the source of the undead plague.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
The adventurers explore Frostholm and its surroundings, gathering clues about the curse. They learn of a forgotten crypt in the nearby Iceclaw Mountains, believed to be the resting place of an ancient sorcerer who once controlled the dead. As they delve deeper into the mystery, they encounter restless spirits, eerie landmarks, and ancient tomes that hint at the sorcerer's dark pact with a malevolent entity.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As they reach the crypt, they are confronted by Valdimar, the tiefling gambler and inventor, who reveals his plan to harness the sorcerer's power for his own gain. A fierce battle ensues, but Valdimar escapes, leaving behind clues about his hideout and his next move. The party must now race against time to stop Valdimar before he can fully awaken the ancient horror.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The adventurers track Valdimar to a ruined fortress at the heart of the frozen wasteland. Here, they face a final showdown against Valdimar and his undead minions. As the battle rages, the ancient sorcerer's spirit begins to awaken, threatening to overwhelm the party with its dark power. They must use their wits and strength to defeat Valdimar and seal the sorcerer's spirit back in its crypt.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With Valdimar defeated and the sorcerer's spirit sealed away, the undead threat subsides. The villagers of Frostholm express their gratitude, and Bruni presents the adventurers with a token of the village's appreciation. Varda and their companions reflect on their journey, knowing they have prevented a great evil from consuming the land.

# Milly Urchin's Folly
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, TheGrym}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In the heart of an eerie wetland, a cursed sanctuary stirs with the restless dead. Milly Urchin, a reckless and inquisitive halfling archaeologist, has unwittingly unleashed an ancient curse, causing the dead to rise. The players must navigate the haunted landscape, confront Milly, and find a way to break the curse before the undead horde spreads beyond the wetlands.
### Hook
The players, independent adventurers, each receive a desperate plea from the nearby village of Eldermoor. The village elder begs for help, claiming that the dead are rising from the wetland and terrorising the villagers. The players are offered a substantial reward to investigate and stop the source of the undead uprising.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Eldermoor Village**: A small, frightened village on the edge of the wetland, where the players first hear of the undead uprising. The village elder provides crucial information and supplies.
2. **Wetland Outskirts**: The initial entry point into the wetland, characterised by foggy marshes and scattered ruins. The players face their first encounter with the undead here.
3. **Ancient Runes**: Deep within the wetland, these runes are carved into ancient trees and stone obelisks, offering clues about the lost civilisation and the curse.
4. **Sunken Sanctuary**: The heart of the wetland, a partially submerged ruin where Milly discovered the cursed artefact. It's a place of dark magic and the showdown.
5. **Abandoned Campsite**: Milly's base of operations, where the players find her journal and other archaeological tools. This location provides background information and a chance to prepare for the final confrontation.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
As the players enter the wetland, they encounter their first wave of undead: shambling skeletons and bloated corpses emerging from the murky waters. They must fight off these initial attackers while navigating the treacherous terrain. The battle is fierce, hinting at the growing danger within the wetlands.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After defeating the undead, the players discover a deeper path into the wetland. Along the way, they find clues about the source of the uprising: ancient runes carved into trees, decayed remnants of a long-lost civilisation, and eerie whispers carried by the wind. They also find Milly's journal detailing her excavation of a sunken sanctuary and her discovery of a powerful artefact.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players finally encounter Milly, who is trying to control the artefact's power. However, her reckless actions have only made things worse. The artefact is a cursed relic, and its power is beyond her control. As the players attempt to help or confront her, a massive undead creature, drawn by the artefact's energy, rises from the depths of the sanctuary.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players must battle the colossal undead creature, a horrifying amalgamation of bones and swamp muck. During the battle, they need to avoid Milly's reckless interference while trying to disable the artefact. The fight is intense and demands both strategy and bravery.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the creature defeated and the artefact disabled, the curse begins to lift. The remaining undead collapse into lifeless heaps, and the eerie atmosphere of the wetland starts to dissipate. Milly, though grateful, is remorseful for her actions. The players decide her fate, whether to turn her over to the authorities, help her make amends, or leave her to her own devices.

2. **Redemption**: The players help Milly find a way to atone for her mistakes. She pledges to protect the wetlands and study its mysteries responsibly. The players receive a rare relic as a token of their gratitude.
3. **Forgiveness**: The players leave Milly to her own devices, believing she has learned her lesson. They return to Eldermoor, where the villagers reward them with gold and supplies. Milly's fate remains uncertain, but she is no longer a threat.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# Xylophone's Vengeance
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, trevpb_101}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
An ancient curse stirs the restless dead in the heart of the treacherous Blackthorn Mountains, unleashing an undead uprising that threatens the nearby villages. Zarina Earthbound, a merciful and wise half-elf baker, is thrust into this dark adventure alongside her vengeful and mysterious Halfling glassblower companion, Mungo Xylophone, and the cunning, adventurous half-orc escort, Feng Axejaw. Their mission: to uncover the source of the curse and end the rising tide of undead. Standing against them is Yelarion Fairpeak, a stoic and cunning half-elf whose impulsive actions have inadvertently triggered this supernatural calamity.
### Hook
The players receive urgent pleas for help from the villagers near the Blackthorn Mountains. The dead have risen, and the villagers believe that only someone brave and skilled can lift the curse. Zarina, Mungo, and Feng find themselves drawn to this mystery, each with personal stakes and secrets intertwined with the cursed land.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Ravenshade Village:** A small, beleaguered village at the base of the Blackthorn Mountains, filled with frightened villagers, simple homes, and makeshift defences against the undead.
2. **Hermit's Hut:** A secluded, ramshackle hut on the outskirts of Ravenshade, filled with ancient tomes and mystical artefacts. The hermit provides crucial information and a map to the players.
3. **Blackthorn Mountains:** Treacherous and foreboding, these mountains are filled with narrow paths, hidden caves, and lurking dangers, including undead patrols and natural hazards.
4. **Ancient Cave:** A dark, labyrinthine cave system within the Blackthorn Mountains. It houses the seals that once contained the curse, now broken and teeming with undead.
5. **Lich’s Sanctum:** The deepest chamber of the ancient cave, where the lich conducts its dark rituals. Filled with necromantic energy, undead minions, and the remains of failed experiments.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The adventure begins with the trio arriving at the village of Ravenshade, situated at the base of the Blackthorn Mountains. The villagers are in a state of panic as the undead relentlessly assault their homes at night. The players must defend the village from a sudden attack by a horde of zombies, showcasing their skills and gaining the villagers' trust.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After fending off the undead, the players are directed to an old hermit living on the outskirts of the village. This hermit, a former scholar of ancient curses, reveals that the source of the undead uprising lies deep within the Blackthorn Mountains. The hermit gives them a map leading to an ancient cave believed to house the curse's origin. The journey through the mountains is fraught with peril, including treacherous paths, traps, and eerie encounters with restless spirits.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
As the players delve into the cave, they uncover evidence that someone has tampered with the ancient seals that kept the curse at bay. They find Yelarion Fairpeak, who confesses that he was trying to harness the curse's power to protect his people but lost control. However, they also discover that Yelarion isn't alone; a powerful lich has been manipulating him to break the seals, intending to use the curse for its own dark purposes. The players must decide to ally with Yelarion or face him as a foe.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The final confrontation occurs in the cave's heart, where the lich is attempting to complete a dark ritual to unleash the curse fully. The players must battle through waves of undead and confront the lich in a climactic showdown. Depending on their earlier decision, Yelarion may either aid them or need to be defeated alongside the lich.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
With the lich defeated, and the ritual stopped, the curse begins to wane. The players must then decide what to do with Yelarion. They can choose to show mercy and help him redeem himself or deliver justice for his impulsive actions. The villagers of Ravenshade are eternally grateful, and the players leave with newfound respect and rewards.

# Fortunes of the Fallen
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::
#### Backer's Name: {{pen,color:black, Dustin Kurtz}}
{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
An ancient curse stirs the undead from their graves in the eerie wetlands known as the Sable Marsh. Katerina Vogel, a travelling fortune teller, and her adventurous son, Mikhail, must uncover the secrets behind the curse and confront the malevolent force responsible. Their journey is fraught with supernatural dangers and dark revelations as they encounter the enigmatic thief, Raum Sable, whose actions have unleashed this undead uprising.
### Hook
The players, independent adventurers, are drawn to the Sable Marsh by rumours of an undead uprising and the promise of great rewards for those who can end it. They cross paths with Katerina and Mikhail, who seek their help to uncover the truth behind the curse and stop the undead menace.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Sable Marsh Edge**: The misty boundary of the marsh, where the players first encounter the undead and meet Katerina and Mikhail.
2. **Abandoned Village**: A desolate settlement within the marsh, filled with eerie silence and clues about the curse.
3. **Ancient Burial Site**: A haunted graveyard where the players encounter restless spirits and uncover hints about the necromancer.
4. **Hidden Tomb Entrance**: A concealed and trap-laden entrance to the tomb where the curse was unleashed.
5. **Tomb’s Deepest Chamber**: The final confrontation site, a dark and foreboding chamber where the players face the necromancer and his minions.

\column
::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The players arrive at the edge of the Sable Marsh, where they encounter a terrified villager fleeing from undead creatures. The villager pleads for their help, explaining that the dead have risen and are attacking the nearby settlements. As the players fend off an initial wave of undead, Katerina and Mikhail appear, offering their aid and explaining their own quest to lift the curse.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
Venturing deeper into the marsh, the players explore eerie, fog-laden landscapes and abandoned villages. They encounter restless spirits, uncover ancient burial sites, and gather clues about the curse's origins. Katerina uses her Tarot cards to divinate their path, revealing cryptic hints about the dark force behind the undead uprising. The players learn of a hidden tomb where the curse was first unleashed.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players find the hidden tomb, but it is guarded by powerful undead and treacherous traps. As they navigate the tomb's dangers, they discover that Raum Sable, a thief with honourable yet stubborn traits, is also seeking the source of the curse. Raum reveals that he unintentionally triggered the curse while attempting to steal a powerful artefact. The players must decide whether to ally with him or view him as an adversary.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
The players confront the malevolent force controlling the undead in the tomb's deepest chamber: a vengeful spirit bound to the artefact Raum sought. The spirit, a powerful necromancer from ages past, seeks to unleash an army of the dead upon the world. The players, alongside Katerina, Mikhail, and possibly Raum, engage in a climactic battle against the necromancer and his minions, using all their resources and wits to prevail.
#### Step 5 - Resolution
The curse is lifted with the necromancer's defeat, and the undead return to their graves. The players emerge from the tomb, weary but victorious. Katerina and Mikhail express their gratitude, and Raum either part ways or joins the group, depending on the players' choices. The villagers are saved, and the marsh begins to heal.

3. **Pyrrhic Victory**: The players fail to fully lift the curse fully, only managing to weaken it temporarily. The undead threat is diminished but not eradicated. The players receive a small reward, and Katerina vows to continue the fight, seeking further help.{{pageNumber,auto}}

# The Sands of the Dead
::
{{margin-top:5px
##### Theme: Gothic Horror
###### Sub-theme: Undead Uprising
}}
::

{margin-top:-5px}
:
### Abstract
In a forsaken desert, an ancient power stirs the dead from their slumber, threatening to engulf the world in darkness. Ysgra Cliffside, a Dwarven messenger, and her companion Dylena Lakeshore, a half-elven banker, are drawn into the heart of this nightmare. Their journey through the cursed sands will reveal buried secrets, test their bravery, and force them to confront an unlikely connection to Zane Truestrike, a mysterious beggar. The players must navigate this eerie landscape, unravel the mystery of the undead uprising, and stop the ancient evil before it consumes them all.
### Hook
Each player receives a letter from an unknown sender offering a significant reward for investigating strange occurrences near a once-sacred desert oasis. The letter hints at a growing threat to the surrounding regions and appeals to their sense of duty, greed, or curiosity. Upon arriving at a small, dying village at the desert's edge, they learn of Ysgra and Dylena’s journey into the desert. They are urged to follow them to uncover the truth and save what remains of the oasis before the undead plague spreads further.
### Location Descriptions
1. **Desert Outskirts:** A desolate region where the players first encounter signs of the undead plague. Scattered bones, tattered tents, and a lone survivor set the ominous tone.
2. **Ghost Caravan:** A spectral caravan that haunts the desert, offering cryptic warnings and riddles to the players. This encounter provides clues about the curse and the oasis.
3. **Ruined Shrine:** A crumbling temple dedicated to a forgotten desert deity. Here, the players can learn more about the history of the oasis and the dark magic corrupting it.
4. **Oasis:** The heart of the one-shot, now a twisted reflection of its former self. Once a place of life and purity, it is now a corrupted, undead-infested nightmare where the final battle takes place.
5. **The Ancient Altar:** An ancient, cracked stone altar at the centre of the oasis, where the skeletal priest prepares the dark ritual. This is the focal point of the players' efforts to stop the resurrection of the dark deity.


::
### Steps
#### Step 1 - Initial Conflict
The players, arriving at the outskirts of the cursed desert, come across a camp ravaged by what seems to be an undead attack. The air is thick with the stench of decay, and the sand is stained with blood. They find a lone survivor, severely wounded, who warns them of the horrors ahead and mentions a dwarf and a half-elf who passed through before the attack. As night falls, the players are ambushed by a horde of skeletons and zombies rising from the sand, forcing them to fight for their lives.
#### Step 2 - Discovery / Exploration
After surviving the attack, the players continue toward the oasis, guided by faint traces left behind by Ysgra and Dylena. Along the way, they encounter eerie remnants of the past: a ghostly caravan, the ruined shrine of a forgotten deity, and a desert merchant who sells cryptic items that may aid them in their journey. As they draw closer, they discover that the oasis was once a holy site, now defiled by dark magic, and that Zane Truestrike, the beggar they’ve heard whispers of, might be connected to the uprising. They also find signs that the undead have captured Ysgra and Dylena, urging them to pick up the pace.
#### Step 3 - Twist / Complication / Fun&Game
The players reach the oasis only to find it overrun with undead. Among them, they spot Ysgra and Dylena shackled and led by a sinister, skeletal priest toward an ancient, cracked altar at the oasis’s centre. The priest seems to be preparing a ritual to awaken something even more powerful and dangerous. As the players plot their next move, they realise that Zane Truestrike is no ordinary beggar; he is the last living guardian of the oasis, cursed for failing to protect it. He has been orchestrating events to bring capable adventurers, like the players, to end the ritual and save the region, though his true intentions remain unclear.
#### Step 4 - Final or Climactic Confrontation
{font-size:16px}
Players must choose to trust Zane or fight him, a skeletal priest, and the undead horde to prevent a ritual that could resurrect a dark deity. The intense confrontation requires players to split their efforts between battling the undead, rescuing Ysgra and Dylena, and stopping the ritual. Success will purify the cursed oasis and reduce the undead plague.
